When enabled, the tool prepares the execution environment, runs the script, and assesses its output against the reported failure. RepGen provides an interactive command-line interface supporting local and hosted LLMs, configurable execution budgets, and inspectable artifacts, along with a GitHub Action for automated issue processing. The underlying approach reproduced 85 of 106 real-world DL bugs (80.19%). A controlled study with 27 developers showed a 23.35% improvement in reproduction success and a 56.8% reduction in reproduction time.

</details>


### [102] [One Verdict Is Not Enough: Regression Testing Learned World Models and Their Test Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41130)
*Junyu Huo,Zihao Wan,Ziqi Mao,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TetraState：面向配对可能/不可能视频测试的回归测试协议，将测试框架纳入被测系统并版本化，以逐测试结果转移（而非模态准确率）作为回归判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型世界模型以软件组件形式发布，模型版本、预处理与测试框架随版本变化。现有负向翻转（negative flips）方法虽能统计逐样本回归，但仍存在两个问题：(1) 测试在一种配置下通过、在另一配置下失败；(2) 测试框架故障可能在无崩溃情况下产生错误分数与错误判定。

Method: 提出 TetraState 协议：每个配对可能/不可能视频测试在四个固定的模型原生配置下运行，依据正确判定数将其归入四类结果；将不确定测试与执行失败与判定率分开报告；以逐测试的结果转移比较版本修订；并把测试框架作为被测系统的一部分进行版本化。

Result: 在 IntPhys 2 Main 全部 506 个测试及 Cosmos 3 Nano、V-JEPA 2 两个异构系统上，两个 V-JEPA 2 框架修订使模态准确率仅变动 0.2 与 2.6 个点，却分别改变 11 与 214 个测试标签；第二次修订中准确率上升但正确判定测试数减少，且 43 个回归仍保持模态判定，故仅检查模态判定翻转会漏检。

Conclusion: 模态准确率无法反映配置级回归，需要以配置级回归判据（oracle）作为学习型组件的评测研究方向；测试框架版本化是必要的。

Abstract: Learned world models ship as software components whose model versions, preprocessing, and test harnesses change between releases. Work on model updates already counts per-sample regressions, or negative flips, that an accuracy check hides. Two problems remain: a test can pass under one configuration and fail under another, and a harness fault can produce erroneous scores and wrong verdicts without a crash. We present TetraState, a regression-testing protocol for paired possible/impossible video tests. Each test runs under four fixed model-native configurations; its number of correct verdicts assigns one of four outcome classes. Inconclusive tests and failed executions are reported separately alongside the verdict rate. Revisions are compared by per-test outcome transitions, and the harness is versioned as part of the system under test. On all 506 IntPhys 2 Main tests and two heterogeneous systems, Cosmos 3 Nano and V-JEPA 2, two V-JEPA 2 harness revisions move modal accuracy by only 0.2 and 2.6 points yet change 11 and 214 test labels; in the second, accuracy rises although fewer tests are judged correctly, and 43 regressions keep the modal verdict, so a modal-verdict flip check misses them. These results outline a research agenda on configuration-level regression oracles for learned components.

</details>


### [103] [Lineage Is Memory: Parent Inheritance and Feedback Disclosure in Short-Budget LLM-Guided Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41157)
*Shanglin Yang,Zhan Shi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过1750次预注册实验，在匹配评估预算下将"父代继承"与"反馈披露广度"解耦，发现贪心式父代继承贡献了迭代优化器绝大部分进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将迭代语言模型优化器的性能瓶颈归因于单轮反思过窄，并提出全量错误聚类、定向小批量、更大提议模型等机制以扩展每轮可见的失败模式；但这些机制始终在保留谱系的搜索中进行测试，导致"父代继承"与"每轮反馈广度"的贡献相互混淆，无法判断性能提升的真实来源。

Method: 在9个提示词与程序合成基准上开展1750次预注册运行，在匹配评估预算条件下对比两类设置：保留父代的贪心谱系搜索，与重置到种子的重采样；并让重置重采样也获得每轮全量失败的聚类摘要或使用更大的提议模型。此外对GEPA的token开销进行分解分析。

Result: 贪心父代继承驱动了绝大部分优化进展，即使种子重采样获得全量失败聚类摘要或使用更大提议模型，仍不及父代继承；全量错误聚类与多样化失败选择仅改善种子出发的单步改写并加速搜索前几轮，但随着已解决错误模式被保留并从后续失败列表中过滤，顺序父代继承迅速弥合该差距；token分解显示提议模型反思仅占总成本很小部分，而验证与小批量门控几乎消耗全部预算。

Conclusion: 先前归因于复杂反思机制的性能增益，实质上主要来自贪心父代继承下对原始失败的利用，其以极小计算代价即可达到复杂反思循环的效果；提示词/程序优化器的设计应更重视谱系保留而非扩大每轮反馈广度。

Abstract: Iterative language-model optimizers refine prompts and programs by evaluating a candidate, showing a proposer model a sample of failing cases, and keeping revisions that improve performance. Recent work attributes the limits of this loop to narrow single-round reflection, introducing full-set error clustering, targeted minibatches, or larger proposer models so the optimizer sees more failure modes per step. Yet every existing evaluation tests these mechanisms exclusively inside lineage-preserving search, where each revision edits a surviving parent, leaving the contribution of parent inheritance confounded with the breadth of per-round feedback. Across 1,750 pre-registered runs spanning nine prompt and program synthesis benchmarks, we separate parent inheritance from feedback disclosure under matched evaluation budgets. We find that greedy parent inheritance drives the vast majority of optimization progress, outperforming seed-reset resampling even when seed-reset proposals receive a clustered summary of all failures in every round or use a larger proposer model. While full-set error clustering and diverse failure selection improve single-step rewrites from the seed and accelerate the opening rounds of search, sequential parent inheritance rapidly closes this gap as solved error modes are retained in the candidate and filtered from subsequent failure lists. Moreover, decomposing token expenditure in GEPA reveals that proposer reflection accounts for only a small fraction of total cost whereas validation and minibatch gating consume nearly the entire budget, showing that greedy parent inheritance over raw failures captures the gains of complex reflective loops at a fraction of their compute.

</details>


### [104] [ROSTrans: Empirically Benchmarking LLMs for ROS1-to-ROS2 Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41168)
*Yichen Zhao,Minghao Shao,Zhuoyan Yu,Yijun Shen,Boyuan Chen,Yik-Cheung Tam,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 ROSTrans——首个针对 ROS1→ROS2 代码迁移的 LLM 基准，含 108 个来自 70+ 开源 ROS1 仓库的任务与可执行参考实现及运行时测试；六个前沿模型 pass@1 均低于 10.9%，56.5% 的样本可编译但运行时失败，执行引导修复可显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代码生成评测多集中于通用或领域任务，机器人软件（尤其 ROS）开发场景鲜有研究。ROS 代码具有节点间协调、中间件配置、生命周期语义等复杂特性，且许多正确性属性只有在节点启动并通信后才可观测，标准静态/编译型 oracle 无法覆盖。

Method: 构建 ROSTrans 基准：108 个 scaffolded completion 任务，源自 70+ 个开源 ROS1 仓库，按 interface-level、behavioral-level、system-level 三级层次分类刻画正确性。每个任务配备可执行参考：已验证的 ROS2 实现、启动节点并覆盖 topics/services/actions/parameters 的运行时测试套件，以及保证可复现的容器。评分时将候选仅生成的目标文件注入固定参考包副本，防止候选篡改或削弱测试；六个前沿 LLM 每任务采样 5 次，并评测执行引导修复（execution-guided repair）。

Result: 六个前沿模型 pass@1 均低于 10.9%；56.5% 的样本编译成功但在运行时失败，其中 32.0% 在迁移接口的运行时断言上失败；执行引导修复经三轮后使任务成功率提升至 Claude Opus 4.6 的 49.1% 与 GPT-5.5 的 47.2%。

Conclusion: ROS 代码迁移对当前 LLM 仍具显著挑战，仅靠编译通过不足以判定正确性，运行时验证必不可少；执行反馈驱动的迭代修复是有效提升路径。ROSTrans 为机器人代码生成提供了可复现、抗作弊的评测框架。

Abstract: Although large language models have shown strong capabilities in domain-specific code generation, their performance in robotics software development, especially for the Robot Operating System (ROS), remains largely unexplored. ROS code generation presents distinctive challenges, including complex inter-node coordination, middleware configuration, and lifecycle semantics that go far beyond standard coding benchmarks, and many important correctness properties become observable only once migrated nodes are launched and communicate. We present ROSTrans, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on ROS1-to-ROS2 code migration, a representative and practically important setting for ROS code generation. ROSTrans comprises 108 scaffolded completion tasks drawn from a diverse set of 70+ open-source ROS1 repositories and organized into a hierarchical taxonomy assessing interface-level, behavioral-level and system-level correctness. For every task we establish an executable reference: a verified ROS2 implementation, a runtime test suite that launches the nodes and exercises their topics, services, actions and parameters, and a container ensuring reproducibility. Candidates are scored by injecting only their target files into an otherwise fixed copy of the reference package, so a candidate can neither supply nor weaken the tests it is judged by. Evaluating six frontier LLMs over five stochastic generations per task, we find pass@1 below 10.9%, and that 56.5% of all samples build successfully and then fail at runtime,and 32.0% fail runtime assertions on the migrated interfaces, a class of error no compile-only oracle can detect. Execution-guided repair further improves task success, reaching 49.1% and 47.2% for Claude Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.5 after three rounds, respectively.

</details>


### [105] [Cognitive Inclusivity in AI Coding Assistants: Evidence from GitHub Copilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41192)
*Grace Kerr,Aarushi Parasrampuria,Faith Culas,Kelly Blincoe*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过20名学生使用GitHub Copilot的实验室研究，结合出声思维与访谈，并用GenderMag框架分析，发现AI编程助手存在5类认知包容性问题，常见交互范式偏向特定认知风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多关注AI编程助手对生产力和代码质量的影响，较少探讨其如何支持不同思维方式；非AI软件工程工具的证据表明工具设计与用户认知风格不匹配会造成包容性障碍，但AI编程助手是否存在类似问题尚不清楚。

Method: 开展20名学生参与的受控实验室研究，使用GitHub Copilot完成代码生成、调试与重构任务；采用出声思维协议、半结构化访谈和归纳式主题分析识别交互模式，再应用GenderMag框架考察不同认知风格间的差异。

Result: 识别出五类包容性问题，分别涉及Copilot如何解释建议、支持验证、促进学习、暴露功能以及影响问题解决过程。

Conclusion: AI编程助手中的常见交互范式可能使某些认知风格占据优势，同时给其他认知风格用户带来额外认知负担；据此提出面向更具包容性的AI编程助手的设计启示。

Abstract: AI coding assistants are commonly used for daily software development tasks. While prior research has examined their impact on productivity and code quality, less is understood about how these tools support diverse ways of thinking. Evidence from non-AI software engineering tools suggests that mismatches between tool design and users' cognitive styles can introduce inclusivity barriers, but it remains unclear whether similar issues arise in AI coding assistants. We investigate how developers with different cognitive styles experience and interact with these tools and whether they exhibit cognitive inclusivity challenges. We conducted a controlled laboratory study with 20 student participants using GitHub Copilot to complete code generation, debugging, and refactoring tasks. Using think-aloud protocols, semi-structured interviews, and inductive thematic analysis, we identify patterns in user interaction. We then apply the GenderMag framework to examine how these patterns differ across cognitive styles. We identify five inclusivity issues concerning how Copilot explains suggestions, supports validation, facilitates learning, exposes its features, and influences the problem-solving process. Our findings suggest that common interaction paradigms in AI coding assistants can privilege certain cognitive styles while introducing additional cognitive effort for others. We derive design implications for more inclusive AI coding assistants.

</details>


### [106] [Yesterday's Code, Today's Dependencies: How Dependency Selection Shapes PHP Migration Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41263)
*Afridi Mahmud,Md. As-Aid Rahman,Rifat Shahriyar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究PHP 7.4→8.3迁移基准中依赖选择策略的影响：使用项目源代码与存档包记录，对比"现代选择"与"发布日选择"两种依赖策略，发现依赖选择不仅改变老项目是否通过测试，还改变基准能观察到的迁移失败类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移基准要求测试在迁移前就通过，但旧项目代码不变也可能因今日重装引入更新库而行为不同，从而改变初始测试结果与迁移中观察到的失败；因此需要考察依赖选择策略如何影响基准有效性与可复现性。

Method: 以PHP 7.4→8.3迁移为对象，固定源码与已保存的包记录，固定Composer 2，比较两种依赖策略：现代选择（允许快照中所有版本）与发布日选择（只允许项目发布日之前发布的版本）；并对已提交lock文件做单独对比；使用共享测试运行器并绕过Composer平台检查以隔离测试行为；对存在差异的项目做针对性干预实验。

Result: 发布日选择在3,660个release上将PHP 7.4通过率提升7.1个百分点，且比现代选择更接近记录中的依赖版本；在两策略下7.4均通过的项目中，PHP 8.3测试结果在15.0%的可用配对里不同，候选迁移任务集仅重叠58.5%；差异多出现在Laravel项目中，旧依赖触发弃用警告而新版本避免；移除这些初始失败后可恢复测试通过或暴露更多不兼容。

Conclusion: 依赖选择不仅决定旧项目是否通过，也决定基准能观察到哪些迁移失败；保留的lock文件是最强的版本参照，缺失时发布日选择是具备历史依据的替代方案；迁移基准应显式声明依赖选择策略并报告其对结果的影响。

Abstract: Migration benchmarks need projects whose tests pass before migration begins. Yet an old project can behave differently without any change to its code. Reinstalling it today may introduce newer libraries, changing both its initial test results and the failures observed during migration.
  We investigate this effect in PHP 7.4-to-8.3 migration. Using unchanged project source and saved package records, we compare two dependency policies. Modern selection allows all versions in the snapshot, while release-date selection allows only versions published by the project's release date. With Composer 2 fixed, release-date selection improves the PHP 7.4 pass rate by 7.1 pp across 3,660 releases. A separate comparison against committed locks shows that it also recovers recorded dependency versions more closely than modern selection.
  The choice matters after migration as well. Among projects that pass on PHP 7.4 under both policies, PHP 8.3 test outcomes differ in 15.0% of eligible pairs. We use a shared test runner and bypass Composer's platform check to isolate test behavior. The resulting candidate migration task sets overlap by only 58.5%. Most differences arise in Laravel projects, where older dependencies trigger deprecations that newer versions avoid. Targeted interventions show that removing these initial failures can restore passing tests or expose further incompatibilities.
  Thus, dependency selection changes not just whether an old project passes, but also which migration failures a benchmark can observe. Preserved locks provide the strongest available version reference; without them, release-date selection offers a historically grounded alternative. Migration benchmarks should make this choice explicit and report its effect on their results.

</details>


### [107] [Architectural Debt Enforcement under Incomplete Observation: A Controlled Study of Ratchets and Evidence Qualification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41276)
*Zhiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"证据资格化"的架构债务门控决策契约，区分观测合格、禁止关系识别、继承债务与授权基线更新四环节，并以240个固定文件与480个构造场景进行受控评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构债务门控需要区分"被允许的变更"与"观测不完整导致的变更"，而现有做法（静态基线、仅计数基线）难以对证据资格作细粒度判定，故需一个明确分离各决策环节的契约化框架。

Method: 将门控决策拆分为观测资格化、禁止关系身份、继承债务、授权基线更新四部分；联合"当前观测器+程序化参考"对240个固定文件做边一致性比对；在480个共享60个源锚点的构造状态场景上，将候选棘轮与成熟依赖巡航基线、静态基线、仅计数基线对照；再叠加一层通用绑定层以验证规格化不匹配的拒绝与合法重复的许可，并用两个有界包作用域做40场景与48个新进程功能检查；辅以诊断解释残余不确定性（类型/运行时关系、安装上下文、生成产物来源），并在TypeScript 5.9.3与6.0.2上做10组配对发射见证。

Result: 240文件中166个（69.17%）被联合资格化且与参考边完全一致，74个仍为reference-UNKNOWN，各仓库覆盖率自13/60至59/60不等；480场景中候选棘轮与dependency-cruiser基线全部匹配预期结果，静态与仅计数基线分别为420与360；但保留失败包括9次额外false-PASS探测与120次未资格化的全树计时尝试；绑定层拒绝54个规格化不匹配、接受54个当前对照并允许合法重复；两个TypeScript版本在10组配对见证上输出一致，但不改变冻结的UNKNOWN标签。

Conclusion: 作者明确不宣称候选方案优越，贡献在于对"证据资格化强制"及其边界的受控刻画，而非语言完备性、自然维护有效性或新的收缩基线算法；残余不确定性由诊断层面予以解释。

Abstract: Architectural debt gates must distinguish an allowed change from an incompletely observed one. We study a decision contract separating observation qualification, prohibited relation identity, inherited debt and authorized baseline updates. Across 240 fixed files from four repositories, the current observer and programmatic reference jointly qualify 166 files (69.17%), all with exact reference-relative edge agreement; 74 remain reference-UNKNOWN. Coverage varies from 13/60 to 59/60 by repository. On 480 constructed state scenarios sharing 60 source anchors, both the candidate ratchet and mature dependency-cruiser baseline match every expected outcome, whereas static and count-only baselines match 420 and 360. These results do not establish candidate superiority. Preserved failures include nine additional false-PASS probes and 120 unqualified full-tree timing attempts. A subsequent generic binding layer rejects 54 specified mismatches, accepts 54 current controls and permits legal repetition. Two bounded package scopes support 40 scenarios and 48 fresh-process functional checks; their costs remain tied to the measured implementations. Diagnostics explain residual uncertainty through type/runtime relations, installation context and generated-artifact provenance. TypeScript 5.9.3 and 6.0.2 yield identical outputs on ten paired emission witnesses, but neither that agreement nor verified release provenance changes the frozen UNKNOWN labels. The contribution is a controlled account of evidence-qualified enforcement and its limits, not language completeness, natural maintenance effectiveness or a new shrinking-baseline algorithm.

</details>


### [108] [What Does a Repair Agent Gain from Remembering Its Failure? An Exploratory Study on Real Python Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41433)
*Wenbo Ye,Yongxi Zhou,Junwei Yao,Jiahui Ge*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在75个BugsInPy缺陷、181次失败尝试上，五种失败记忆条件的重试增益点估计均高于朴素重试（+5.5~+9.9个百分点），但均不显著；模型自写"教训"相对原始记录无优势，且结局在失败实例间的方差远大于条件间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体修复失败后被重试时，常附带失败记录（对话记录transcript、自写教训lesson、提取的测试信号）。现有实践普遍假设这些"失败记忆"有用，但缺乏对其究竟改变了重试中什么的严格检验。

Method: 在75个BugsInPy缺陷上，对gpt-5.4-mini的181次失败尝试各做重试，设五种条件，并预先注册分析（偏差均报告）；两项预先注册的后续实验用更大的gpt-5.4重复朴素重试/记录/教训三条件，并新增仅告知失败（notice-only）的对照组。以bug-revealing测试判定成败，共12项预先声明的对比。

Result: ①各记忆条件相对朴素重试的点估计增益+5.5~+9.9点（基线29.8%），均不显著。②自写教训相对其所概括的记录为-1.7点（95% CI [-8.7,+4.9]；gpt-5.4为-3.5），却多耗一次模型调用。③仅告知失败无显著效果，而完整记录比notice多解决12.2点（CI [+4.2,+20.0]，部分运行156次失败），为12项对比中唯一显著者，且在gpt-5.4上不显著。④事后分析：带记忆的重试更少出现"未修改任何Python文件"就结束，编辑类重试触及参考修复所改文件的比例接近天花板。⑤事后全测试套件检查：曾见上次测试结果的重试更常弄坏参考修复可通过的其他测试，削弱其表面增益。⑥结局在失败实例间的差异远大于条件间差异。

Conclusion: 失败记忆带来的重试增益在当前样本量下无法与噪声区分；压缩式"教训"不优于原始记录，因此不建议以额外模型调用换取教训。记忆值不值得其成本取决于条件效应的真实大小，作者给出了确证性研究所需的样本量。研究支持"记忆内容本身（而非仅失败信号）才可能起作用"这一方向性线索，但需更大规模重复验证。

Abstract: When a coding agent fails a repair task, it is often retried with a record of the failure: the transcript, a self-written lesson, or extracted test signals. We ask what such failure memory changes in the retry. On 75 BugsInPy bugs, we retried each of 181 failed attempts of gpt-5.4-mini under five conditions, with a predeclared analysis whose deviations we report. Two predeclared follow-ups repeated the plain-retry, transcript and lesson conditions with the larger gpt-5.4 and added a notice-only control. On the bug-revealing tests, every memory condition gained over a plain retry in point estimate (+5.5 to +9.9 points over 29.8%), but none significantly. Five further findings stand out. First, a model-written lesson showed no advantage over the transcript it summarises (-1.7 points, 95% CI [-8.7, +4.9]; -3.5 for gpt-5.4), although it costs an extra model call. Second, the transcript may do more than tell the agent that it failed: a notice alone produced no significant change, while the transcript resolved 12.2 points more failures than the notice for gpt-5.4-mini (CI [+4.2, +20.0], partial run of 156 failures; the only significant one of twelve predeclared contrasts, and not significant for gpt-5.4). Third, post hoc, fewer retries with memory ended without modifying any Python file, while the share of editing retries that touched a file the fix changes was near ceiling. Fourth, in a post hoc full-test-suite check, retries that had also seen the previous attempt's test results more often failed other tests that the reference fix passes, which shrinks their apparent gains. Fifth, outcomes varied far more between failures than between conditions, and we give the sample sizes a confirmatory study would need.

</details>


### [109] [Post-SQLFix: Property-Guided Repair of LLM-Generated SQL with Runtime Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41472)
*Zhihao Guo,Zonghan Wu,Yilei Shao,Haizhou Du,Nan Tang,Xiao Cheng,Huan Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 Post-SQLFix，将 Text-to-SQL 修复重构为性质引导的程序修复，用分层静态分析器与时间监控器取代不可靠的执行反馈式修复，确定性提升修复保真度与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-SQL 修复依赖执行反馈（EF），其错误信息是症状级、方言相关的，迫使 LLM 猜测根因；同时存在"仲裁悖论"——系统既用未经验证的模型提出修复，又用它判断修复是否满足所需性质。

Method: 提出 Post-SQLFix：先把 SQL 规范化方言感知的规范查询结构（CQS），再经三层分层静态分析器（语法层、上下文无关结构层、上下文敏感语义层）校验并产出精确的、模式感知的违规诊断以指导 LLM 修复；配合 Verify-Before-Apply 时间监控器，仅接受能减少违规数、不重新引入已修复违规、且满足算子契约的修复；运行时不变式受声明规则族与算子契约约束，有限重试预算保证停机。

Result: 在 BIRD、Spider、Mini-Dev 三个基准、3000+ 评测实例、三种 SQL 方言（SQLite/MySQL/PostgreSQL）与六种模型配置上，Post-SQLFix 修复了 EF 遗留执行错误的 77.8%，修复迭代次数不到一半、token 减少 30%；监控器下被接受的转换未引入静态可检测的结构回归，检查过的修复保真度转化为最高 +11.6 个百分点的绝对准确率提升。

Conclusion: 以性质引导的静态验证替代症状级执行反馈，可消除仲裁悖论，在更少迭代与更低 token 成本下显著提升 Text-to-SQL 修复的有效性与正确性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) for Text-to-SQL frequently generate queries with syntax errors, invalid identifier references, or violations of SQL scoping and typing rules. Repairing these errors is a program correctness problem, yet existing approaches rely on Execution Feedback (EF), whose symptom-level, dialect-specific error messages force the LLM to speculate on root causes. We identify an Arbitration Paradox in this paradigm: the system relies on unverified models both to propose repairs and to make the final judgment on whether those repairs satisfy the required properties. We resolve this paradox by reformulating Text-to-SQL repair as property-guided program repair. Our framework, Post-SQLFix, canonicalizes SQL into a dialect-aware Canonical Query Structure (CQS) and validates it through a hierarchical static analyzer with three stratified property layers (Syntactic, Context-Free Structural, Context-Sensitive Semantic). The analyzer produces precise, schema-aware violation diagnostics to guide the LLM's repair proposals. A Verify-Before-Apply Temporal Monitor then accepts only repairs that reduce the violation count, do not reintroduce resolved violations, and satisfy the operator contracts. These runtime invariants are bounded by the declared rule family and operator contracts, while a finite retry budget ensures halting. On three established benchmarks (BIRD, Spider, Mini-Dev) spanning over 3,000 evaluation instances, three SQL dialects (SQLite, MySQL, PostgreSQL), and six model configurations, Post-SQLFix resolves 77.8% of the execution errors that EF leaves unrepaired, while requiring less than half as many repair iterations and 30% fewer tokens. Crucially, accepted transitions under the monitor introduce no statically detectable structural regressions in our evaluation, translating checked repair fidelity into absolute accuracy improvements of up to +11.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [110] [SCRBench: Beyond Equivalence Checking for Semantic Change Reasoning in Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41562)
*Omid Saghatchian,Ali Mahzoun,Khashayar Etemadi,Saeed Reza Kheradpisheh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出SCRBench（67,658对Python程序），围绕"语义等价检测、语义变更诊断、代码变更序列重建"三项任务评测语言模型的代码推理能力，发现高等价准确率会高估模型对代码语义变化的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码推理评测多用"两程序是否语义等价"的yes/no判断，无法揭示模型是否知道是哪些代码变更导致了语义差异，或哪些变更序列生成了修改后的程序，因此需要更细粒度的诊断性评测。

Method: 构建SCRBench：通过对程序施加保持或改变语义的代码变更序列，生成67,658对Python程序；在4,500对分层子集上评测5个语言模型，设置三项任务——语义等价检测、语义变更诊断、代码变更序列重建。

Result: 等价检测对弱模型有区分度，准确率随变更累积而下降，但最强模型接近上限；诊断任务暴露被等价准确率掩盖的缺陷（等价准确率94.71%的模型仅诊断出54.44%的语义变更）；序列重建最难，最佳模型仅39.27%完全重建正确，施加5处变更时无一模型超过1.11%。

Conclusion: 高语义等价准确率会高估模型对代码变更语义效应的推理能力，需引入诊断与重建类任务进行更严谨的评测。

Abstract: Understanding how code changes affect program semantics is a core aspect of code reasoning in language models. It is commonly evaluated by asking whether two programs are semantically equivalent, but a yes/no answer does not reveal whether a model knows which code change caused a semantic difference, or which sequence of code changes produced the modified program. We study two tasks that ask these questions directly, diagnosing semantic change and reconstructing sequences of code changes, alongside detecting semantic equivalence. We build SCRBench, 67,658 Python program pairs generated by applying sequences of code changes that preserve or alter program semantics, and evaluate five language models on a stratified subset of 4,500 pairs. Detecting semantic equivalence exposes weaker models, whose accuracy declines as changes accumulate, but the strongest models remain near the ceiling. Diagnosing semantic change reveals weaknesses that equivalence accuracy hides: a model with 94.71% equivalence accuracy diagnoses only 54.44% of semantic changes. Reconstructing sequences of code changes is much harder: the best model reconstructs the exact sequence for only 39.27% of pairs, and no model exceeds 1.11% when five changes are applied. High equivalence accuracy therefore overestimates how well models reason about the semantic effects of code changes.

</details>


### [111] [Prompt Less, Infer More: Static Analysis-Guided LLM Type Annotation for Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41617)
*Wonseok Oh,Michael Pradel,Hakjoo Oh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SALT：静态分析引导的LLM类型推断系统，在12个真实Python项目上取得最高准确率，并将LLM成本最多降低78.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python类型注解可提升可维护性与可靠性，但实际项目普遍注解不足，人工标注耗时易错。现有神经/LLM类型推断在准确率与效率间难以兼顾：或未能利用项目级类型证据，或因冗余模型调用与噪声提示而低效。

Method: SALT将静态分析与LLM推断结合：先用静态分析解析可直接求解的类型，据此对LLM查询进行优先级排序，并构建简洁的、感知类型的上下文提示，从而减少不必要的模型调用与提示噪声。

Result: 在12个真实Python项目上，SALT在所评测系统中准确率最高；相较强编码助手基线，估计LLM成本最多降低78.5%；在前沿模型GPT-5.5上表现出相近的成本节省，说明其有效性可泛化到强LLM。

Conclusion: 以静态分析引导LLM、按需查询并精简上下文的混合式设计，可在保持高类型推断准确率的同时显著降低LLM成本，适用于不同强度的LLM。

Abstract: Type annotations improve the maintainability and reliability of Python programs, but many real-world projects remain under-annotated because manual annotation is time-consuming and error-prone. Recent neural and LLM-based type inference systems can recover missing annotations, yet they often trade accuracy for efficiency. Existing approaches often fail to exploit project-wide type evidence, or do so inefficiently through unnecessary model calls and noisy prompts. We present SALT, an LLM-based type inference system guided by static analysis. SALT integrates static analysis with LLM inference to resolve types statically, prioritize LLM queries, and construct concise type-aware context. To demonstrate the effectiveness of SALT, we evaluated it on 12 real-world Python projects. SALT achieves the best accuracy among the evaluated systems and reduces estimated LLM cost by up to 78.5% compared with a strong coding-assistant baseline. It also shows similar cost savings with the frontier model GPT-5.5, demonstrating that SALT remains effective with strong LLMs.

</details>


### [112] [What Carries Architectural Signal? A Code-to-Architecture Mapping Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41628)
*Rakhshanda Jabeen,Morgan Ericsson,Yuyao Duan,Anna Wingkvist*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了代码到架构映射任务中文档表示与图上下文的作用，发现目录规则已能提供极高基线（89-100%），而成员节点等图结构扩展无稳定收益，警示需先厘清表示与目录组织已贡献的信息再归因于图神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码表示可编码文本、依赖与目录结构，但预测性能的提升本身不足以证明额外图处理（如邻居聚合、成员/文件夹节点）的必要贡献——依赖信息可能已隐含在表示中，目录结构也可能与参考架构分解高度吻合。作者据此质疑对图神经网络的归因。

Method: 在10个Java、C、C++系统上以有限标注预算开展实验。比较词汇、结构、上下文三类文件表示；对每个预测器在有/无依赖邻居情况下评估；采用异质图变换器（HGT），对比文件级图加入成员节点、文件夹节点、二者兼有，以及将文件夹路径直接编码进表示的变体；并将文件夹感知预测与使用相同标注文件的目录规则进行对照。

Result: 编码依赖信息的表示在多数系统上优于纯词汇表示；邻居聚合的效果依预测器与聚合方向而异，可能带来提升；文件夹感知变体在各HGT配置下增益最大且最一致。但目录规则本身达到89-100%的系统级中位准确率，且对规则误分类的文件加入文件夹信息并未带来一致改善；成员节点相对文件级图无稳定提升。

Conclusion: 在将改进归因于图神经网络之前，应先评估文件表示与目录组织本身已提供了什么。目录规则是强基线，成员节点扩展收益有限；依赖表示的价值主要体现在作为输入特征而非额外图聚合。

Abstract: Code-to-architecture mapping assigns source files to modules of a reference architecture. Representations of implementation artifacts can capture code text, dependencies, and directory organization, but predictive gains alone do not establish the contribution of additional graph processing. Dependencies may already be encoded in these representations, while directories may closely match the reference decomposition. We investigate how artifact representations and graph context affect mapping on ten Java, C, and C++ systems with a limited labeling budget. We compare lexical, structural, and contextual file representations and assess each predictor with and without dependency neighbors. Using a Heterogeneous Graph Transformer (HGT), we compare file-level graphs extended with member nodes summarizing each file's program elements, folder nodes, or both, alongside a variant encoding folder paths directly in file representations. We compare folder-aware predictions with a directory rule using the same labeled files.
  Representations encoding dependency information outperform lexical representations on most systems. Additional neighbor aggregation can improve mapping, depending on the predictor and aggregation direction. Folder-aware variants yield the largest and most consistent gains across the evaluated HGT configurations. However, the directory rule achieves per-system median accuracy of 89-100%, and adding folder information does not consistently improve predictions for files the rule misclassifies. Member nodes provide no consistent improvement over file-level graphs. These findings support assessing what file representations and directory organization already provide before attributing improvements to graph neural networks.

</details>


### [113] [A Failure Mode Taxonomy and Severity Model for Ethereum Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41685)
*Fernando Richter Vidal,Naghmeh Ivaki,Nuno Laranjeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文面向以太坊智能合约，基于Nickerson等的分类法开发方法构建了一套故障模式分类法（五个维度：根因、违反的安全属性、影响、受影响组件、性质），并据此提出五级严重性模型CIPER；经系统文献综述、OpenSCV漏洞目录及后续事故的饱和性检验未发现新故障模式或维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约不可篡改，部署后故障后果严重且不可逆；已有工作侧重漏洞、缺陷与攻击的分类，而合约部署后“可观察到的故障”（偏离预期行为或账本状态）长期被忽视，缺乏统一的描述词汇与严重性度量。

Method: 以文献综述为输入，采用Nickerson等人的分类法开发方法推导故障模式及其维度；每个故障模式沿根因、违反的安全属性、影响（主/次/其他）、受影响组件、性质五个维度刻画，并采用面向可观察 malfunction 而非底层漏洞的标准化命名；进而由分类法导出五级严重性模型CIPER，按可观察影响对故障模式分组；最后以系统文献综述、OpenSCV漏洞目录和搜索截止后报告的智能合约事件进行饱和性检验。

Result: 饱和性检验未产生新的故障模式或维度，表明所构建的以太坊智能合约故障模式分类法在覆盖范围上达到饱和，并被验证有效。

Conclusion: 该分类法为描述智能合约“失败时做错了什么”提供了共享词汇，CIPER则将该词汇与每种可观察后果的严重程度关联起来，为智能合约故障的描述、评估与比较提供统一框架。

Abstract: Smart contracts are a core component of blockchain systems, but their immutability makes failures in deployed code catastrophic and irreversible. Existing work classifies vulnerabilities, defects, and attacks, yet the observable failures of smart contracts, the deviations from intended behavior or the expected ledger state seen once a contract is deployed, have been largely disregarded. In this paper, we design and validate a failure mode taxonomy for Ethereum smart contracts, together with a severity model that ranks failures by their observable impact. Using a literature review as input, we follow the taxonomy development method of Nickerson et al. to derive the failure modes and their dimensions, and we subject the resulting taxonomy to a saturation exercise over a systematic literature review, the OpenSCV vulnerability catalog, and smart contract incidents reported after the search cutoff. Each failure mode is characterized along five dimensions, namely root cause, violated security property, impact (recorded as primary, secondary, and other), affected component, and nature, and is given a standardized name targeting the observable malfunction rather than the underlying vulnerability. From the taxonomy, we derive CIPER, a five-level severity model that groups failure modes by their observable impact. The saturation exercise produced no new failure modes or dimensions. The taxonomy offers a shared vocabulary for describing what smart contracts do wrong when they fail, and CIPER links that vocabulary to the severity of each observable outcome.

</details>


### [114] [Reparo: Synthesizing and Validating Access Control Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41800)
*Ayesha Binte Mostofa,Gehao Zhang,Sam Stern,Juan Zhai,Pubali Datta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Reparo 是一个 LLM 辅助的访问控制策略合成、修复与验证流水线，利用仓库证据引导 LLM 生成初始策略，结合官方校验工具自动修复语法错误，并用另一 LLM 从相同证据构造行为预言机进行语义验证。在 228 个使用 Cedar、OpenFGA、Rego 的 GitHub 仓库上，Reparo 将合法策略比例从原有的 40.6–55.7% 失败率提升至 99.0–100% 合法，与预言机一致率达 76.3–99.6%，人工复核确认 91.5–96.9% 测试用例正确，单仓库耗时约 5 分钟、成本 0.55–1.57 美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为软件应用编写正确的访问控制策略很困难，需要掌握授权语言并深入理解应用的授权需求。现有合成与挖掘方法假设存在显式授权输入和可信的参考策略，但实际中授权逻辑分散于代码、注释和文档中，且真实仓库中开发者定义的策略往往不完整、过时或错误，因此缺乏可靠参考策略成为核心障碍。

Method: Reparo 分三阶段：(1) 合成——从仓库中收集反映授权意图的证据，据此引导 LLM 生成初始策略；(2) 修复——调用官方策略语言校验工具定位错误并自动修复；(3) 语义验证——由另一个（不同的）LLM 基于同一仓库证据推导出行为预言机，即包含具体授权请求及预期 allow/deny 决策的测试集，并在生成策略上执行。此外，对预言机在 390 个测试用例的统计样本上进行人工评估。

Result: 在 228 个使用 Cedar、OpenFGA、Rego 的真实 GitHub 仓库上评估：原始仓库策略中有 40.6–55.7% 无法通过语言校验工具，而 Reparo 修复后产出 99.0–100% 合法策略；与预言机一致率为 OpenFGA 98.2–99.6%、Cedar 82.0–86.5%、Rego 76.3–80.2%；人工复核确认抽样测试用例中 91.5–96.9% 正确；单仓库合成与验证约耗时 5 分钟，成本 0.55–1.57 美元。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使不存在可靠的参考策略，仓库证据也能同时支撑访问控制策略的合成与独立验证。

Abstract: Writing correct access control policies for software applications is difficult because it requires expertise in the authorization language and a thorough understanding of the application's authorization requirements. Existing synthesis and mining approaches assume explicit authorization inputs and a trusted reference policy. However, authorization logic is often distributed across code, comments, and documentation rather than captured in a single artifact, and developer-defined policies in real-world application repositories are often incomplete, outdated, or incorrect. We present Reparo, an LLM-aided pipeline for access control policy synthesis, repair, and validation. Reparo collects repository evidence for authorization intent and uses it to guide an LLM in generating an initial policy. It then uses official policy-language validation tools to identify and automatically repair errors. For semantic validation, a different LLM derives a behavioral oracle from the same repository evidence, consisting of concrete authorization requests with expected allow or deny decisions, which Reparo executes against the generated policy. We manually assess the oracle on a statistically sized sample of 390 test cases. We evaluate Reparo on 228 real-world GitHub repositories that use Cedar, OpenFGA, or Rego. In these repositories, 40.6-55.7% of the repository policies fail their language's validation tool, whereas Reparo produces 99.0-100% valid policies after repair. Agreement with the oracle is 98.2-99.6% for OpenFGA, 82.0-86.5% for Cedar, and 76.3-80.2% for Rego. Manual review confirms 91.5-96.9% of the sampled test cases as correct. Synthesizing and validating the policy for one repository takes about five minutes and costs $0.55-$1.57. These results show that repository evidence can support both access policy synthesis and independent validation, even when no reliable reference policy exists.

</details>


### [115] [Stuck on Red? Understanding GitHub Actions Failing Streaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41863)
*Aref Talebzadeh Bardsiri,Tom Mens,Alexandre Decan,Thomas Degueule,Jean-Rémy Falleri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对30.9M+次GitHub Actions运行进行实证研究，刻画工作流"连续失败链"（failing streaks）的开始、结束与演化规律，并按用途分类工作流、抽样分析日志，揭示失败并非都源于最近提交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub Actions是GitHub仓库事实上的CI/CD服务，其工作流不应在非预期情况下失败，也不应长时间持续失败。作者希望实证理解失败链的成因与生命周期，从而为维护者、GitHub平台与工具建设者提供可操作的改进建议。

Method: 基于43K+仓库、154K+工作流、238天内30.9M+次运行的大规模实证分析：定义"失败链"为不间断的连续失败运行序列；结合每次运行的触发器（trigger）与提交（commit）分析失败链如何开始与结束、期间发生什么；将所有工作流按用途（构建、测试、部署等）分类；并对小样本失败链的日志进行人工检查（含100条失败链的抽样分析）。

Result: 失败多以链式出现：75%的失败运行紧随另一次失败；至少3次运行的工作流中半数以上存在至少一条失败链。承担多种用途的工作流失败链更频繁（同等规模下亦然）。多数失败链在数小时内结束，但占五分之一的超过1天的长失败链贡献了工作流失败时间的主要部分。三分之一失败链的开始与五分之二的结束均无任何仓库变更。100条样本中，无变更结束的失败链多因网络问题或外部服务故障。此外，"相同规模下多用途工作流更易失败"这一结论需注意工作流复杂度等混杂因素。

Conclusion: 维护者不应默认失败源自最近一次提交。GitHub与工具建设者可标记"无仓库变更却失败"的运行，并在工作流持续失败超过一天时提醒活跃维护者。

Abstract: GitHub Actions is the de facto CI/CD service for GitHub repositories. Its workflows should not fail when they are not expected to, nor keep on failing too long. We empirically study failing streaks, i.e., uninterrupted series of failing runs of a workflow, in 30.9M+ runs of 154K+ workflows of 43K+ GitHub repositories over 238 days. We study how failing streaks begin and end, and what happens during them, based on the trigger and the commit of each run. We also classify all workflows by their purposes, such as building, testing or deploying, and we inspect the logs of a small sample of failing streaks. Failing runs mostly come in streaks: three quarters of them follow another failing run, and half of the workflows with at least three runs have at least one failing streak. Workflows serving multiple purposes have failing streaks more often, even at the same size. Most failing streaks end within hours, but the one in five that last more than a day account for the lion's share of the time that workflows spend failing. A third of the failing streaks start, and two in five end, without any change to the repository. In a sample of 100 failing streaks, those that end this way most often fail because of network issues or an external service. These findings suggest that maintainers should not assume that a failure comes from the last commit. GitHub and tool builders could flag failures that occur without any change to the repository, and alert the active maintainers when a workflow stays failing for more than a day.

</details>


### [116] [No More Import Errors: Constrained Decoding for Guaranteed Valid Imports in LLM-Generated Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41878)
*Konstantinos Kitsios,Matteo Biagiola,Guido Salvaneschi,Alberto Bacchelli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出约束beam解码方法CBDL，在解码阶段保证LLM生成测试的import合法，并解决import重复导致的退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM测试生成流水线最常见的失败原因之一是import错误。语法约束解码虽可保证import合法，但会引发退化解，即重复的合法import（import streaks）耗尽token预算，导致测试未能生成。

Method: 提出Constrained Beam Decoding with Lookup（CBDL）：在解码阶段对最终层概率施加语法约束以保证import合法，同时通过lookup与beam搜索避免过早锁定将导致重复import的token；方法位于解码层，可无缝集成现有流水线。实现覆盖Python与Java。

Result: 生成的测试import合法率达100%，优于RAG、LIBRO与Agentic Repair（分别遗留45%、10%、43%的import错误）；平均将14%的初始import错误转化为功能正确的测试，整体正确率提升1–4个百分点；在修复流水线中释放修复预算，Python提升2–7个百分点、Java提升1–6个百分点；小模型+CBDL生成import、GPT-5.2生成测试体的混合方案比纯GPT-5.2高1–3个百分点；开销为每测试16–21秒。

Conclusion: CBDL以较小开销消除LLM测试生成中的import瓶颈，既提升测试正确性，又支持低成本的混合生成范式。

Abstract: One of the most common failure reasons of LLM-based test generation pipelines is import errors. Grammar constrained decoding can modify the probabilities of the LLM's final layer during token generation to guarantee valid imports. However, we find that it can lead to degenerate solutions, i.e., repetitive valid imports (import streaks) that exhaust the token budget before a test is generated. We propose Constrained Beam Decoding with Lookup (CBDL), a novel method that guarantees import validity while addressing import streaks by avoiding premature commitment to tokens that lead to repetitive imports. Because it operates at the decoding stage, CBDL can be integrated into existing LLM-based test generation pipelines.
  We implement CBDL for Python and Java and evaluate it on two test generation tasks, showing that generated tests have 100% valid imports, outperforming both static (RAG and LIBRO) and dynamic (Agentic Repair) import-validity approaches that leave 45%, 10%, and 43% of import errors unresolved. CBDL converts on average 14% of initial import errors into functionally correct tests, increasing overall test correctness by 1-4 percentage points over unconstrained generation.
  Even when import validity does not directly lead to a correct test, it allows agentic repair pipelines to allocate the repair budget to remaining errors, increasing correct tests by 2-7 percentage points for Python and 1-6 for Java. Finally, we show that a hybrid pipeline, where a small local model equipped with CBDL generates imports while the frontier GPT-5.2 model generates the test body, outperforms generating both with GPT-5.2 alone by 1-3 percentage points.
  Overall, CBDL removes a major bottleneck in LLM-based test generation: by guaranteeing import validity with a small overhead (16-21 seconds per test), it improves test correctness and enables cost-effective hybrid generation.

</details>


### [117] [Dittany: Contribution-Guided Repair Framework for MAS Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42144)
*Hanqi Sun,Yuchen Shao,Hanyi Si,Beibei Xu,Lisheng Wang,Chengcheng Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 contribution-guided 运行时修复框架 \tool，用于 LLM 多智能体系统（MAS）应用执行失败后的自动修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的 MAS 应用常产生有用的中间结果，但任务需求未被满足；修复此类执行需同时识别仍待完成的工作与可复用的已完成结果。

Method: 从任务需求与方法规范中推导期望贡献（expected contributions）与接受条件（acceptance conditions），以贡献图（contribution graph）连接其依赖关系；运行时检查定位未满足条件并判定哪些结果仍可用；据可用输入选择动作级修正（action-level correction）或图级恢复（graph-level recovery），两种修复范围均保留已验证贡献，并在后续工作使用前校验修复输出。

Result: 在 6 个 MAS 框架、30 个应用、9,000 个任务上评估：运行时故障检测精确率 88.28%、召回率 79.18%；任务准确率 84.76%，较 Native 执行提升 26.74 个百分点，较四种恢复基线中最优者提升 21.28 个百分点；估计部署 token 消耗较 Native 高 17.92%，但较 Reflexion 低 24.19%、较 Iterative Rollback 低 13.05%。

Conclusion: 结合动作级与图级两种修复范围，相比仅用图级修复可获得更高准确率与更低 token 消耗；\tool 在准确率与成本上均优于现有恢复基线。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent system (MAS) applications can produce useful intermediate results while leaving task requirements unsatisfied. Repairing such executions requires identifying the work still needed and the completed results that can support it. We present \tool, a contribution-guided runtime repair framework for MAS applications. \tool derives expected contributions and acceptance conditions from task requirements and method specifications, and connects their dependencies in a contribution graph. Runtime checks locate unmet conditions and establish which results remain usable. These results guide action-level correction when available inputs suffice, or graph-level recovery when prerequisites or unfinished work need revision. Both repair scopes retain validated contributions and check repaired outputs before subsequent work uses them. We evaluate \tool on 30 applications across six MAS frameworks and 9,000 tasks. It detects runtime failures with 88.28\% precision and 79.18\% recall, and achieves 84.76\% task accuracy, improving over Native execution by 26.74 percentage points and the strongest of four recovery baselines by 21.28 points. Estimated deployment token consumption is 17.92\% above Native, but 24.19\% below Reflexion and 13.05\% below Iterative Rollback. Ablation results show that combining the two repair scopes achieves higher accuracy and lower token consumption than graph-level repair alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Automatic Construction of Strong Simple Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40700)
*Caleb N. Ellington,Jiaqi Wang,Sohan Addagudi,Elijah Cole*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用大语言模型自动构建基线，以检验科学领域深度学习方法的真实增益；在36个来自Nature/Science/Cell系期刊的任务上，自动基线可恢复中位数95.5%的"平凡预测器—深度模型"性能差距，12项研究中简单基线达到或超过原深度模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在物理与生命科学中日益普及，但大量所谓"进展"实为缺少或遗漏基线所导致的假象；而人工同行评审难以跟上文献增长速度，缺乏可扩展的基线核查手段。

Method: 从Nature、Science及Cell系同行评审文献中收集36个监督式分类与回归任务（均为面向科学应用的新型深度学习方法）；用语言模型为每个任务自动构建简单基线；以"平凡预测器得分与深度模型得分之间的差距"为标尺衡量自动基线的强度，并分析跨学科领域、发表期刊的差异，以及测试时算力和研究上下文可得性水平的影响。

Result: 自动构建的基线表现强劲：中位数恢复95.5%（均值85.7%）的差距；中位数情形下简单基线在12项研究中匹配或超过深度模型性能；并刻画了不同科学领域、发表venue以及test-time compute与上下文层级带来的变化。

Conclusion: 自动化基线构建是一种实用且可扩展的检验手段，可用于核查物理与生命科学中深度学习贡献的真实有效性，弥补人工评审在规模上的不足。

Abstract: To rigorously establish the value of a novel deep learning method, it must be compared against appropriate baselines. As deep learning has become increasingly common in the physical and life sciences, there have been numerous cases in which apparent progress has turned out to be an artifact of omitted or missing baselines. In this work, we investigate whether this problem can be mitigated by using large language models to automate the construction of baselines. We assemble 36 supervised classification and regression tasks from peer-reviewed studies in Nature, Science, and Cell-family journals that investigate novel deep learning methods for scientific applications. We then use a language model to construct simple baselines for each task. These automatically constructed baselines are strong, recovering a median of 95.5% (mean 85.7%) of the gap between a trivial predictor and the score of the deep learning model. The median simple baseline matches or exceeds the performance of the deep learning model in 12 studies. We further examine the variation across scientific domains and publication venues and we characterize the effects of test-time compute and access to different levels of study context. As human peer review has struggled to keep up with the rapidly increasing volume of research articles in recent years, our results suggest that automated baseline construction is a practical and scalable check on deep learning contributions in the physical and life sciences.

</details>


### [119] [Kernel-based Interpolatory Methods for Context Compression of Attention Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40943)
*Michel Fabrice Serret,Laura Grigori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将注意力上下文压缩问题在核函数对应的Hilbert空间中形式化，利用注意力可表示为两个线性映射之比（其插值之比仍为插值）的性质，提出基于GEIM与Q-DEIM的GAIM、Q-DAIM两种KV-cache压缩方法，并用Taylor级数、Performer、RFF等代理模型将复杂度降至线性，同时扩展到流式注意力场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是现代语言模型的基本构件，但其复杂度随上下文长度呈二次增长，成为瓶颈；因此上下文压缩（即KV-cache压缩）受到越来越多关注。作者希望从理论上刻画注意力压缩问题，并据此设计高效、有理论依据的KV-cache压缩方法。

Method: 先把注意力问题在与其核对应的Hilbert空间中形式化：注意力可写成两个线性映射之比，且其插值也等于两个插值之比，从而可直接对线性映射做插值。基于此提出GAIM（广义经验插值法GEIM）与Q-DAIM（Q-DEIM）两种KV-cache压缩方法；由于二者代价随上下文长度二次增长，进一步引入Taylor级数、Performer与随机傅里叶特征（RFF）等代理模型使压缩在线性时间内完成；并将结果推广到流式注意力：允许依据已插值的上下文对插值节点选择施加惩罚，并基于新信息更新先前的插值。

Result: 给出关于残差收敛性以及未来查询输出误差的数值结果；在LongBench-E基准上的初步结果显示性能与现有最先进方法相当。实验结果还表明，Q-DAIM方法所揭示的“范围（range）”可能有助于识别信息在KV cache中集中的位置，从而实现高效压缩。

Conclusion: 以Hilbert空间插值视角统一刻画KV-cache压缩是可行且有效的：GAIM/Q-DAIM及其线性时间代理版本、流式扩展在收敛性与下游基准上表现良好，Q-DAIM的range刻画为定位KV cache中的信息集中区域提供了新工具。

Abstract: The bottleneck of attention mechanisms, which are the fundamental building block of modern language models, is their quadratic complexity in terms of context size. There has thus been an increasing interest in the compression of the context, also called KV-cache compression. In this article, we consider the problem of compressing the context of attention mechanisms by first formulating it in the Hilbert space associated with its kernel. In this formalism, attention can be represented as the ratio of two linear maps whose interpolant is also the ratio of interpolants, this allows us to consider the interpolation of the linear maps directly. We thus introduce two novel KV-cache compression methods which we call GAIM and Q-DAIM, based on the Generalized Empirical Interpolation Method (GEIM) and the Q-Discrete Empirical Interpolation Method (Q-DEIM), respectively. As the cost of these methods is quadratic in terms of context length, we propose surrogate models based on the Taylor series, the Performer and the Random Fourier Features method which allow the compression to be done in linear time. Finally, we extend this result to the streaming attention setting, providing new methods which allow one to penalize the selection of interpolation nodes based on the previously interpolated context and to update the previous interpolation based on new information. We then provide numerical results on the convergence of the residuals and the output error in future queries and preliminary results on the LongBench-E benchmark showing performance comparable to the state-of-the-art methods. These experiments suggest that the range revealed by our Q-DAIM method could help identify where information is concentrated in the KV cache, thus enabling an efficient compression.

</details>


### [120] [Bayesian Distribution System State Estimation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40728)
*Nan Lin,Peter Palensky,Pedro Vergara Barrios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PoSE：用流匹配神经网络只学习变电站注入的先验分布，潮流方程、拓扑与量测仅在采样阶段通过噪声感知的Kalman型校正进入，从而保持DSSE的贝叶斯结构与物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中压馈线实时量测稀少，DSSE依赖二次变电站伪量测（贝叶斯先验）。WLS虽保持潮流精确但把先验退化为均值与方差；有监督深度估计器虽学习潮流，却与特定量测集绑定，且不确定性校准差。

Method: PoSE（posterior sampling for state estimation）保留DSSE的贝叶斯结构，仅学习DSO未知的变电站注入分布：神经网络以flow matching在历史数据上训练为先验；潮流方程、电网拓扑与量测只在采样时通过噪声感知的Kalman型校正进入。故网络不近似潮流，但每个后验样本都是物理一致运行点，且一个训练好的先验可服务任意量测集、噪声水平与拓扑。

Result: IEEE 33节点：全部12种设置下CRPS较全协方差高斯先验降低6.7–7.5%；90%区间覆盖率接近精确后验采样，而有监督估计器仅37–78%。240节点不平衡三相Iowa馈线：注入、电压与支路电流误差均最低。快速变体每快照0.2–0.6s达同等精度；在7种重配置拓扑上无需重训练即优于高斯先验与重训练的有监督估计器。

Conclusion: 将先验学习与物理/量测推理解耦，可在保持物理一致性与可靠不确定性的同时获得跨量测配置与拓扑的泛化能力，优于现有WLS先验与有监督深度估计器。

Abstract: Medium-voltage feeders have few real-time measurements, so distribution system state estimation (DSSE) rests on pseudo-measurements of the secondary substations, a prior in Bayesian terms. Weighted least squares keeps the power flow exact but reduces this prior to a mean and variance, while supervised deep learning estimators also learn the power flow and are tied to one measurement set. We propose PoSE (posterior sampling for state estimation), which keeps the Bayesian structure of DSSE and learns only what the distribution system operator does not know, namely the distribution of substation injections, modelled by a neural network trained with flow matching on historical data. The power flow equations, grid topology and measurements enter only at sampling, through a noise-aware Kalman-type correction. The neural network therefore never approximates the power flow, but every posterior sample is a physically consistent operating point, and one trained prior serves any measurement set, noise level and topology. On the IEEE 33-bus feeder, PoSE lowers the probabilistic injection error (CRPS) by 6.7-7.5% against a full-covariance Gaussian prior in all 12 tested settings. It provides reliable uncertainty: the 90% intervals contain the true injection almost exactly as often as exact posterior samples would, whereas those of supervised estimators contain it in only 37-78% of cases. On the 240-bus unbalanced three-phase Iowa feeder, it gives the lowest injection, voltage and branch-current errors of all estimators. A fast variant matches this accuracy at 0.2-0.6s per snapshot and, without retraining, beats Gaussian priors and retrained supervised estimators on seven reconfigured topologies.

</details>


### [121] [General Frequency Principle for Linear Approximation Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41179)
*Yujun Wang,Yiqi Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在有限线性逼近类中研究梯度下降训练的广义频率原则：证明高频处学习器相对目标幅度在全训练过程中一致趋于零，并给出低频优先的充分条件；但该原则并不意味低频总收敛更快，在特定比较条件下某分量学习时间更长，通常正是较高频分量。Legendre与三角基数值实验验证理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有频率原则（F-Principle）多描述"低频先学"，但缺乏对有限线性逼近类的统一刻画，且"低频优先"与"低频收敛更快"常被混为一谈，需要澄清二者的区别与成立条件。

Method: 针对有限线性逼近类，在适当假设下分析梯度下降学习动态：证明高频处学习器幅度相对目标幅度在全训练过程一致趋零；建立低频优先的充分条件，覆盖单频目标单独训练与双频分量同轮训练情形；推导两分量学习时间的比较条件。

Result: 高频幅度相对目标一致消失；给出低频优先的充分条件；并表明低频优先不推出低频收敛更快——满足比较条件时某分量学习时间更长，常见情形下为较高频分量。Legendre与三角基数值实验与理论一致。

Conclusion: 频率原则应理解为频谱上的幅度/优先性规律而非收敛速率排序；低频优先需附加条件，高频收敛慢是常见但非必然结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a general frequency principle under gradient descent training for the finite linear approximation classes. Under suitable assumptions, we prove that the learner amplitude relative to the target amplitude vanishes at high frequencies, uniformly throughout training. We also establish sufficient conditions for low-frequency priority, covering single-frequency targets trained separately and two frequency components trained in the same run. This general frequency principle does not imply that lower frequencies always converge faster. Instead, for two frequencies that satisfy certain comparison conditions, one component will take longer to learn than the other; and in common cases, the longer component has higher frequency. Numerical experiments with Legendre and trigonometric bases validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [122] [Don't Just Impute, Detect: Task-Aware Diffusion Pipelines for Edge Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40732)
*Jeongsu Park,Taehyeon Yun,Geonwoo Kim,Yuji Lim,Moohong Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出任务感知扩散精修器TADR，用冻结下游检测器的损失景观训练条件扩散模型，缓解缺失块填补中的"填补偏差"，SMD上F1提升0.338。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IIoT边缘流水线常因网络中断与硬件故障丢失连续多传感器数据块。传统"先填补再检测"的分离式流程中，填补器仅以MSE训练，会把缺失块补成最可能的正常模式：部分观测的异常被平滑回正常，正常数据上的边界伪影又引发误报。作者称之为填补偏差（imputation bias）。

Method: TADR为条件扩散精修器，针对冻结下游检测器的损失景观进行训练。由于沿采样轨迹反传不可行，任务感知项作用于噪声预测已给出的一步干净窗口估计上，因此训练开销不高于普通扩散训练。含异常增强目标等任务感知项；10步采样，单CPU核41.4ms/窗口，模型4.03MB，约支持24路并发1Hz流。

Result: 严格Point-to-Point评测、5个随机种子并配对显著性检验：SMD上F1较未精修检测器提升0.338（p=0.0015）；消融中仅异常增强目标贡献0.463。增益随连续且相互冗余通道的数量及中断所移除的异常证据而变化；在由one-hot命令通道主导的遥测数据集和PSM上无增益。

Conclusion: 填补偏差是分离式填补-检测流程的关键缺陷，任务感知扩散精修可在几乎零额外训练成本下显著改善异常检测；其有效性依赖数据的连续冗余结构与中断所损失的异常证据，并非普适，且具备边缘端实时部署可行性。

Abstract: Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) edge pipelines routinely lose contiguous blocks of multi-sensor data to network dropouts and hardware faults. The standard remedy is a disjointed "impute-then-detect" pipeline whose imputer is trained only for Mean Squared Error (MSE). Such an imputer fills a missing block with the most probable normal pattern: a partially observed anomaly is smoothed back toward normality, and boundary artifacts on normal data raise false alarms. We call this imputation bias and propose the Task-Aware Diffusion Refiner (TADR), a conditional diffusion refiner trained against the loss landscape of a frozen downstream detector. Because back-propagating through the sampling trajectory is intractable, the task-aware terms act on the one-step clean-window estimate that the noise prediction already yields, so training costs no more than plain diffusion training. Under strict Point-to-Point evaluation over five seeds with paired significance tests, TADR raises F1 by $0.338$ over the unrefined detector on SMD ($p = 0.0015$), and the anomaly enhancement objective alone contributes $0.463$ in ablation. We also characterise when the method helps: the gain tracks the continuous, mutually redundant channels available and the anomaly evidence the outage removes, and it is absent on telemetry datasets dominated by one-hot command channels and on PSM. At ten sampling steps TADR runs in $41.4$ ms per window on a single CPU core, sustaining about $24$ concurrent 1 Hz streams from a $4.03$ MB model. Code and per-seed results are available at https://github.com/int-park422/TADR.

</details>


### [123] [Unsupervised Feature Selection Using Local Intrinsic Dimensionality -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40736)
*Oussama Chelly,Michael E. Houle,Ken-ichi Kawarabayashi,Muhammad Rajabinasab,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于局部内在维度(LID)的无监督特征选择方法LIDFS：转置数据、以特征为点，用k近邻距离MLE估计各特征LID，低LID得分高，并做去冗余精化，输出完整排序，时间复杂度O(d²n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维性损害学习算法的效率与效果，且许多应用缺少类别标签，无法用监督方式指导降维，因此需要有效的无监督特征选择方法。

Method: 基于LID与距离度量不可区分性的形式等价性；转置数据矩阵使每个特征成为样本点，由到k个最近邻特征的距离用最大似然估计每个特征的LID；LID越低表示局部可区分性越强、重要性得分越高；随后精化步骤在每个特征邻域保留一个代表并降低局部冗余特征；最终给出完整特征排序，任意数量特征可选而无需重跑；不使用标签或类别数信息。

Result: 在23个高维数据集（classic基准集）上与经典及先进方法及随机基线比较；以5个下游分类器的分类准确率和AUC、以及聚类准确率和NMI评估，覆盖完整与激进选择范围；LIDFS在监督评估中排名第一，在聚类评估中排名第一或并列第二，与需要类别数的方法同处最优统计组，且运行快于最接近的竞争者；部分近期方法甚至未能超过随机基线；敏感性分析显示小邻域尺寸效果最佳。

Conclusion: LIDFS是一种无需标签的高效无监督特征选择方法，性能具竞争力且速度快，小k值邻域表现最佳，可作为通用特征排序工具。

Abstract: High dimensionality degrades both the efficiency and the effectiveness of learning algorithms, and in many applications no class labels are available to guide dimensionality reduction. This paper proposes LIDFS, an unsupervised feature selection method based on Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID). The method builds on the formal equivalence between LID and the indiscriminability of distance measures. The data matrix is transposed so that each feature is treated as a point, and the LID of each feature is estimated by maximum likelihood from the distances to its $k$ nearest neighboring features. Features with lower LID exhibit greater local discriminability and receive higher importance scores. A refinement step then retains one representative per feature neighborhood and demotes locally redundant features. The result is a complete ranking from which any number of features can be selected without rerunning the procedure. LIDFS does not use any information about labels or the number of classes, and runs in $\mathcal{O}(d^2 n)$ time for $n$ instances and $d$ features. LIDFS is compared with classic and state-of-the-art methods and a random baseline on 23 high-dimensional datasets, the classic benchmark collection. The evaluation uses classification accuracy and AUC with five downstream classifiers, as well as clustering accuracy and normalized mutual information, over both full and aggressive selection ranges. LIDFS ranks first in the supervised evaluations and first or tied second in the clustering-based evaluations. It belongs to the top statistical group together with methods that require the number of classes, and it runs faster than its closest competitor. Several recent methods, by contrast, fail to outperform the random baseline. A sensitivity analysis shows that small neighborhood sizes yield the best results.

</details>


### [124] [Certified Behavioral Retention for Continual Adaptation with Held-Out Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40740)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq,Abrar Shahid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CABA：一种与候选生成器无关的更新后门控机制，用预留的历史锚点在冻结表征上验证更新是否超出声明的行为改变预算，并给出配套的认证理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续适应中，关键在于判断是否提交更新，而非仅研究如何更新：需要在不对受保护行为造成超过预算的改动的前提下完成适应。现有方法缺乏可审计的、与具体候选策略无关的保留性校验。

Method: 将历史观测从候选优化中隔离作为锚点；在冻结表征上比较旧策略与候选策略，计算最坏锚点前向KL散度；选取在预算内最大的插值系数（网格搜索）。理论部分将测得的锚点保留性与离锚点条件保证分离，在表征覆盖与Lipschitz假设下证明被接受的更新在受保护支撑上满足一致行为散度界，并给出仿射softmax头的正则性界、概率覆盖与压缩保证、插值单调性条件、网格分辨率界以及轨迹级与序列组合保证。

Result: 在UCI HAR作为非平稳传感器策略代理的受控实验中，预算ε=0.05时平均遗忘率从无约束适应的1.01%降至0.00%，但最终平均准确率由93.04%降至88.48%。理论给出锚点保留到受保护支撑一致行为散度界的认证链条。

Conclusion: CABA为暴露可比策略分布与已声明更新路径的候选生成器提供可审计的保留性检查，体现保留—可塑性权衡；但实验仅为示意性代理，不构成基础模型或机器人性能结论，部署仍需另行证明当前任务效用。

Abstract: Continual adaptation requires deciding not only how to update a model, but whether to commit an update without exceeding a declared budget for changes to protected behavior. We introduce Certified Anchor-Based Adaptation (CABA), a candidate-agnostic post-update gate that reserves historical observations from candidate optimization, compares old and candidate policies on frozen representations, and selects the largest tested interpolation coefficient whose worst-anchor forward KL divergence remains within budget. The finite check requires neither action labels nor complete trajectories. We develop a theory-led certification framework that separates measured anchor retention from conditional off-anchor guarantees. Under explicit representation-coverage and Lipschitz assumptions, an accepted update satisfies a uniform behavioral-divergence bound on protected support. The analysis gives regularity bounds for affine softmax heads, probabilistic coverage and compression guarantees, interpolation monotonicity conditions, grid-resolution bounds, and trajectory-level and sequential-composition guarantees. In a controlled UCI Human Activity Recognition study used as a non-stationary sensor-policy proxy, CABA reduces mean forgetting from 1.01 percent for unconstrained adaptation to 0.00 percent at budget epsilon = 0.05, while reducing final average accuracy from 93.04 percent to 88.48 percent. The experiment illustrates a retention-plasticity trade-off and does not establish foundation-model or robotic performance. CABA provides an auditable retention check for candidate generators exposing comparable policy distributions and a declared update path, while deployment also requires separate evidence of current-task utility.

</details>


### [125] [AutoHebb: Self-Modifying Neural Networks that Zero-Shot Infer and Continually Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40757)
*Daniel Levine,David van Dijk,Kenneth Kay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AutoHebb——一类在线学习前馈神经网络，通过元学习固定权重、外部反馈与内部活动生成“神经调质”全局信号，门控三因子Hebbian局部学习规则，从而同时具备强归纳偏置与自主学习控制；在0-shot推理(ZI)与持续学习(CL)任务上成功、涌现出未训练的ZI/CL能力、可逆向工程为显式算法，并优于现代学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类与动物在全新情境下具备0-shot推理(ZI)与持续学习(CL)能力，但现有建模存在两大局限：一是缺乏脑内关键成分——用于ZI的强归纳偏置与用于CL的学习自主控制；二是模型复杂度与表达力难以平衡，成分过复杂则缺乏简洁性、难以分析，过于任务定制则缺乏表达力。

Method: 构建AutoHebb：在线学习的前馈神经网络，不依赖梯度下降等外部控制优化，而是将外部反馈与内部生成的活动结合为全局学习信号（“神经调质”）回传网络；该信号门控一个生物可信的局部学习规则（三因子Hebbian规则）以更新可塑性权重；并用元学习搜索固定的非可塑性权重。

Result: AutoHebb网络(1)在ZI任务上成功；(2)涌现出未经训练的ZI/CL能力；(3)可逆向工程为显式算法；(4)在ZI/CL任务上优于现代学习模型；与相关模型的对比表明元学习、Hebbian可塑性、学习自主控制三者均不可或缺。

Conclusion: AutoHebb兼具简洁性与表达力，可作为生成假设、分析与发现生物可信学习与泛化机制的有效工具。

Abstract: Humans and animals have a unique capacity to generalize and learn in novel circumstances, including "0-shot" inference (ZI) and continual learning (CL) -- two powerful cognitive abilities. Despite this importance, efforts to model ZI and CL are seriously limited in several ways. First, existing models typically lack components critical to ZI and CL in the brain, namely, strong inductive biases (for ZI) and autonomous control of learning (for CL). Second, models can be less tractable to analysis if model components are overly complex (lacking simplicity), or too task-restricted if model components are overly bespoke (lacking expressivity). Here we introduce autonomous Hebbian networks ("AutoHebb"), a class of online learning feedforward neural networks that realize strong inductive biases and autonomously controlled learning, and are both simple and expressive. Unlike standard models that learn via externally controlled optimization (e.g. gradient descent), AutoHebb networks combine external feedback with internally generated activity to produce a global learning signal ('neuromodulator') fed back to the network. The neuromodulator in turn gates a biologically grounded local learning rule (3-factor Hebbian rule) that updates networks' plastic weights. Using meta-learning to search for fixed (non-plastic) weights, we find that AutoHebb networks (1) succeed on ZI tasks, (2) show emergent (untrained) ZI/CL abilities, (3) admit reverse-engineering into explicit algorithms, and (4) outperform modern learning models on ZI/CL tasks. Lastly, performance comparisons to related models implied an essential role for three model components: meta-learning, Hebbian plasticity, and autonomous control of learning. These findings indicate that AutoHebb may be useful in hypothesis generation, analysis, and discovery of biologically grounded forms of learning and generalization.

</details>


### [126] [Frozen in Time: How a Physics Foundation Model Forgets Under Sim-to-Real Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40857)
*Haaris Mian,Miles Cranmer,Alessandro Favero,Payel Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究科学基础模型Walrus-1.3B在两阶段sim-to-real微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，发现少量回放（1%模拟数据）可显著缓解遗忘并保持对实验数据的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学基础模型（SciFM）在多种PDE任务上表现优异且可通过微调适应新数据分布，但微调存在灾难性遗忘风险，即适应新任务会损害模型已有知识。作者希望系统研究SciFM在sim-to-real适应过程中的遗忘现象及其成因。

Method: 以面向连续介质动力学的SciFM模型Walrus-1.3B为对象，设计两阶段sim-to-real适应：先在圆柱绕流模拟数据上微调，再适应同一系统的实验数据。定量测量预训练数据集上的rollout性能退化与单步误差增长，并分析数据集依赖性和任务表征相似性等潜在遗忘驱动因素；随后在真实数据微调时引入少量模拟数据回放（replay）策略。

Result: 在模拟数据上微调仅20个epoch即导致原始预训练数据集上的单步误差最高增加35倍，rollout性能迅速退化；随后对真实数据的适应进一步侵蚀模拟任务性能。在真实数据微调中回放仅1%的模拟数据即可恢复大部分丢失的模拟性能，同时保持对实验动力学的有效适应。

Conclusion: 灾难性遗忘是SciFM进行sim-to-real适应的实际障碍；轻量级回放策略能显著提升知识保留，是缓解该问题的有效且低开销手段。

Abstract: Scientific foundation models (SciFMs) have achieved remarkable performance on a wide range of PDEs, including the ability to efficiently adapt to unseen data distributions with additional finetuning. As with all neural architectures, finetuning such models comes with the risk of catastrophic forgetting, whereby adapting to new tasks degrades prior knowledge of the model. We study catastrophic forgetting in Walrus-1.3B, a SciFM for continuum dynamics, over the course of a two-stage sim-to-real adaptation: first finetuning on simulated flow around a cylinder and then adapting to experimental data of the same system. We find that finetuning on the simulations rapidly degrades rollout performance on the original pretraining datasets, with one-step errors increasing up to 35x after just 20 epochs. Subsequent adaptation to real data further erodes performance on the simulated task. We characterize the potential drivers of forgetting, such as dataset dependence and task representation similarity. We then show that replaying as little as 1% simulated data during real-data finetuning recovers most of the lost simulation performance while retaining effective adaptation to the experimental dynamics. Our results identify catastrophic forgetting as a practical obstacle to sim-to-real adaptation of SciFMs and show that lightweight replay strategies significantly improve retention.

</details>


### [127] [FARO: Function-Space Adaptive Return-to-Risk Optimization via Cross-Block Equal-Risk-Contribution Budget Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40858)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FARO将参数块的划分作为优化决策单元：用反事实回报为每个块分配几何，冻结路由后按跨块等风险贡献(ERC)重分配预算，在GPT+FineWeb上复现人工先验并稳健降低损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型训练配方依赖人工先验，按层类型手工分配优化器（注意力/MLP矩阵用正交化动量，其余用Adam），并共享单一全局更新预算，缺乏原则性依据。

Method: FARO为函数空间自适应优化器：FARO-Adaptive依据各参数块的实现反事实回报为其选择几何；随后冻结路由，按组合理论中的风险平价原则——跨块等风险贡献(ERC)——重新分配冻结的块级预算。

Result: FARO-Adaptive在全调度下达到3.2780 nats，匹配最佳手工配方NorMuonAndAdam(3.2794)；FARO-ERC在全部6个种子上均降低干净数据损失，平均降0.0136 nats，符号检验、配对检验与置信区间一致，超过运行间噪声且无额外时间/内存开销；在损坏数据流下仍降低平均损失并赢得多数种子。

Conclusion: 该框架无需手工设定逐层几何即可恢复人类先验，并通过ERC稳健获得小幅增益；其机制是削减风险最高的块而非扩张最安全的块。

Abstract: Training a large language model is compute-bound, and current recipes divide the work between optimizers by hand: orthogonalized momentum for attention and MLP matrices, Adam-style updates for the rest. This division rests on human priors about which layer type deserves which geometry, and it fixes a single global update budget that every parameter block shares. We introduce FARO, a function-space adaptive optimizer that makes the partition of parameters into blocks the unit of decision. FARO gives each block a geometry by its realized counterfactual return, then freezes the routes and reallocates the frozen per-block budget by cross-block equal risk contribution (ERC), the risk-parity principle from portfolio theory. On a GPT-style model trained on FineWeb, the framework gives two results. First, its FARO-Adaptive arm reaches 3.2780 nats at the full schedule, matching NorMuonAndAdam (3.2794), the best hand-designed recipe: it recovers the human prior without hand-setting per-layer geometry. Second, FARO-ERC improves on that arm: it lowers the clean-data loss at every one of 6 seeds, by 0.0136 nats on average, a modest but robust gain. Its sign test, paired test, and confidence interval all agree, and it exceeds the measured run-to-run noise at no added time or memory. It works by trimming the riskiest blocks rather than expanding the safest, and it holds up under a corrupted stream, still lowering the mean loss and winning the seed majority. The source code is available at https://github.com/wxj630/FARO.

</details>


### [128] [Near-Optimal First-Order Regret for Bandits with Feedback Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40861)
*Mengxiao Zhang,Yuheng Zhang,Haipeng Luo,Paul Mineiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在有向反馈图的对抗老虎机中，弥合了最坏情况与一阶遗憾保证之间的图依赖差距，给出期望遗憾 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\alpha L^\star}+\alpha)$，与强可观测图的最优极小极大率 $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{\alpha T})$ 在阶数上匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果存在图依赖差距：强可观测图上极小极大遗憾为 $\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{\alpha T})$（$\alpha$ 为独立数），而最好的一阶界为 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{(\kappa+1)L^\star})$（$\kappa$ 为团划分编号，$\kappa+1\ge\alpha$，$L^\star$ 为最优臂累积损失）。作者希望得到图依赖为 $\alpha$ 的一阶保证。

Method: 先在自感知图（每顶点有自环）上设计算法：把 EXP3 的更新与受"优化探索"（exploration by optimization）启发的采样规则结合；构造一个映射，其不动点给出采样分布，可用学习器损失与图的独立数来控制单轮遗憾余项；并证明几何收敛的迭代可在多项式时间内算出足够精确的不动点。再将算法推广到一般强可观测图，采用 [LLZ20] 的两级聚合（two-level aggregation）方法。

Result: 得到期望遗憾界 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\alpha L^\star}+\alpha)$，在自感知图与一般强可观测图上均成立；不动点可通过几何收敛迭代在多项式时间内近似计算。

Conclusion: 该结果消除了最坏情况保证与一阶保证在独立数依赖上的差距，使一阶界在 $\alpha$ 的阶上与极小极大最优率一致，且算法具有多项式时间可计算性。

Abstract: We study first-order regret guarantees for adversarial bandits with a fixed, known directed feedback graph. For strongly observable graphs, [ACBDK15] establishes a minimax regret of $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{αT})$, where $α$ denotes the independence number of the graph and $T$ denotes the horizon. On the other hand, the best existing first-order bound scales as $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{(κ+1) L^\star})$ [LLZ20], where $κ$ denotes the clique partition number, satisfying $κ+1\geqα$, and $L^\star$ denotes the cumulative loss of the best arm, leaving a gap between the graph dependence in worst-case and first-order guarantees. In this paper, we bridge this gap by obtaining an expected regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{αL^\star}+α)$. Our first result is an algorithm for the special case of self-aware graphs, in which every vertex has a self-loop. The algorithm combines the update of EXP3 [ACBFS02] with a sampling rule inspired by exploration by optimization [LS20]. More specifically, we construct a map whose fixed points yield sampling distributions that control the one-round regret remainder in terms of the learner's loss and the graph's independence number, which is crucial in deriving optimal first-order guarantees. We further show that a sufficiently accurate fixed point can be computed in polynomial time by a geometrically convergent iteration. Finally, we extend the algorithm to general strongly observable graphs and achieve the same $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{αL^\star}+α)$ guarantee by adapting the two-level aggregation approach of [LLZ20].

</details>


### [129] [TensorSSM: Memorizing Spatial Position in Dynamic State Space for Tensor Core Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40871)
*Sheng Zhang,Xinyi Wang,Maolin Wang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TensorSSM，一种基于隐式神经表示（INR）的功能张量分解方法，将空间坐标记忆于动态状态空间模型（SSM）中，通过 K 叉规则粗到细分块编码与跨模状态映射，提升压缩表达力与存储效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维张量存储开销巨大；现有功能张量分解方法将高阶张量展平为 1D 序列，导致局部坐标序列高度重叠、难以捕捉复杂跨模空间依赖，表达力受限。

Method: 1) 按 K 叉规则将各模坐标分解为序列，以粗到细方式编码空间 token；2) 扩展 SSM 状态空间以显式建模跨模空间依赖，将前一隐状态的跨模信息融入当前状态转移，在序列维（粗到细 token）与状态维（跨模记忆）两个方向扩展张量表示。

Result: 大量实验表明 TensorSSM 具有显著更高的重建精度与存储效率，在多样张量数据集上表现稳健表达力，且不依赖严格的空间假设。

Conclusion: 将空间信息有效融入状态空间与张量核心，提升了状态自适应性与压缩表达力，为高效功能张量分解提供了新范式。

Abstract: High-dimensional tensors are ubiquitous in real-world applications but lead to massive storage overhead. Functional tensor decomposition addresses this by employing neural networks to map element coordinates to tensor cores, significantly reducing the parameters for data compression. However, existing functional methods typically flatten high-order tensors into 1D sequences. This folding operation causes highly overlapping local coordinate sequences and struggles to capture complex cross-mode spatial dependencies, severely limiting their expressiveness on diverse datasets. To tackle these challenges, we propose TensorSSM, a novel Implicit Neural Representation (INR) for functional decomposition method that memorizes spatial positions within a dynamic State Space Model (SSM). Specifically, TensorSSM first decomposes mode-wise coordinates into sequences spit by K-ary rule, encoding spatial tokens from coarse to fine, which effectively captures spatial variations. Furthermore, we extend the SSM's state space to explicitly map cross-mode spatial dependencies, allowing inter-mode interactions to enhance the updates of each mode. By integrating cross-mode information from previous hidden states into the current state transitions, TensorSSM effectively expands tensor representation along two dimensions: the sequence dimension for coarse-to-fine spatial tokens, and the state dimension for cross-mode memorization. This design effectively integrates spatial information into the state space and the tensor core, substantially advancing the state adaptability and enhancing the expressiveness of the data compression. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TensorSSM achieves significantly high reconstruction accuracy and storage efficiency, exhibiting robust expressiveness across diverse tensor datasets without relying on strict spatial assumptions.

</details>


### [130] [TSFLab: A Self-Improving Agent-Agnostic Workbench for Time Series Forecasting Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40901)
*ChengAo Shen,Yanjun Zhao,Haoxin Liu,Bonnie Chen,Miles Ma,Dongjin Song,Hanghang Tong,Qingsong Wen,Jingrui He,Jingchao Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TSFLab——一个自改进、与具体编码智能体无关的"工作台"，通过共享资源与工具让编码智能体低成本地完成时间序列预测(TSF)的复现、基准评测和新方法开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSF文献与产品激增，但约20%的arXiv TSF论文未公开代码链接，已开源代码也需人工维护，导致研究者需反复自行实现；独立实现之间实验设置不一致、难以维护。直接使用Claude Code、Codex等编码智能体成本高、难以扩展到大型研究项目（如基准评测），且缺乏自我维护能力。

Method: 设计TSFLab：一个agent-agnostic的"workbench"，为兼容的编码智能体提供共享资源与工具，支持低成本TSF研究与研究资源的持续维护，并具备自改进能力；实验覆盖复现、基准评测与新方法开发三类任务，与现有编码智能体基线对比。

Result: 在复现与基准评测中，TSFLab使任务完成率提升20–163%，每个完成任务消耗的新增token减少11–66%；在智能体驱动方法开发的初步研究中，预测精度提升，且相比其他设置减少32.8–73.6%的新增token和9.2–68.8%的开发时间。

Conclusion: 共享资源与工具的"工作台"式设计可有效提升编码智能体在TSF研究中的完成率与效率，并降低token成本与开发时间，为智能体辅助基准评测乃至autoresearch提供了可行路径。

Abstract: As publications and products on time series forecasting (TSF) proliferate, researchers face a growing burden of analyzing existing work. First, not all publications release code. For instance, only about 20% of arXiv TSF papers have a code link in the surveyed period. Second, released code also requires manual maintenance. Thus, TSF researchers must constantly adapt or implement existing works. However, independent implementations can differ in experimental settings and are difficult to maintain. We therefore propose to use AI agents for benchmarking TSF research and even supporting autoresearch, e.g., developing new TSF models. Yet directly applying existing coding agents, such as Claude Code and Codex, is suboptimal: prohibitive costs, limited scalability to large research projects (e.g., benchmarking), and lack of self-maintenance. To address these challenges, we propose TSFLab, a self-improving agent-agnostic "workbench" that equips compatible coding agents with shared resources and tools for low-cost TSF research and continuous maintenance of research resources. Our experiments cover reproduction, benchmarking, and new method development. In reproduction and benchmarking, TSFLab improves task completion by 20-163% and cuts fresh tokens per completed task by 11-66% across baselines. A pilot study of agent-driven method development also shows improved forecasting accuracy via TSFLab, with 32.8-73.6% fewer fresh tokens and 9.2-68.8% less development time than the other settings.

</details>


### [131] [No Channels Left Behind: Understanding Diffusion Acceleration through Residual Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40914)
*Junha Park,Jaemyung Yu,Jaehui Hwang,Jong-Seok Lee,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 扩散Transformer的训练加速方法（如REPA与弥散正则）的效果部分源自对AdaLN-Zero残差门控路径（α）的重组，而非仅来自辅助目标所施加的特征结构；据此提出的无教师径向目标可提升门参与度并改善FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释将训练加速归因于额外监督、空间结构先验或特征几何改善，但本文重新审视后发现AdaLN-Zero残差门α被显著重组，从而提出"加速至少部分来自残差门控通路重塑"的假设，并希望找到可量化的加速轴与更简洁的加速手段。

Method: 1）观察REPA、弥散正则等方法下α的显著变化；2）验证加速模型对α扰动更敏感，且门几何反映类别语义关系；3）提出归一化参与率比（nPR）刻画门控的通道维组织；4）基于"更广门参与对应更优生成质量"的发现，设计无教师径向目标，通过特征范数正则促进更广门参与，无需成对特征比较。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256、SiT-B/2上，该方法相较弥散损失降低FID，并提升跨时间步与类别的平均门参与度；在多种REPA教师、投影器架构与径向目标设定下，更广的门参与均与更好生成质量相关。

Conclusion: 残差门控重组是扩散训练加速的一个可测量维度，为加速方法设计与分析提供了简单且有前景的方向。

Abstract: Training acceleration methods for diffusion transformers, such as REPA and dispersive regularization, are commonly explained by additional supervision, spatial-structure priors, or improved feature geometry. We revisit this view and uncover a striking internal change: the AdaLN-Zero residual gate $α$, which directly controls how conditioned updates enter the residual stream, is substantially reorganized by these methods. This observation motivates our hypothesis that acceleration arises, at least in part, from reshaping the residual gating pathway rather than solely from the feature structures explicitly imposed by auxiliary objectives. Supporting this view, accelerated models are markedly more sensitive to perturbations of $α$, and their gate geometry reflects semantic relationships among classes. We further introduce the normalized participation ratio (nPR) to characterize the channel-wise organization of the gates and find that, across REPA teachers, projector architectures, and radial-objective targets, broader gate participation accompanies better generation quality. Guided by this insight, we propose a simple teacher-free radial objective that promotes broader gate participation through feature-norm regularization, without pairwise feature comparisons. On ImageNet $256\times256$ with SiT-B/2, our method improves FID over dispersive loss while increasing mean gate participation over timesteps and classes, further supporting the link between gate organization and training acceleration. Our results identify residual-gate reorganization as a measurable axis of diffusion training acceleration and suggest a simple direction for further acceleration.

</details>


### [132] [A Theoretical Framework for Understanding and Building "Any-task inference" Capable Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40934)
*Matthew McDermott,Florent Pollet,Payal Chandak,Gregory Kondas,Liat Friedman Antwarg,Noa Dagan,Isaac Kohane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为"任意任务推理"（ATI）基础模型给出形式化框架与数学定义，提出"任务空间"概念与基于接口的定义，通过"通用任务归约"视角将模型分解为编码器、模型与解码器，并据此分解模型误差为"设计项"与"实现模型超额项"，最后给出生成式与判别式两类原型实现方案，并以5个真实案例佐证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作缺乏对"零样本式任意任务推理"基础模型的正式数学刻画，常与"预训练-微调"范式混为一谈；作者希望提供严格定义，以便分析模型能否达到最优性，并厘清误差来源。

Method: 1）将"感兴趣的任务空间"纳入问题域形式化；2）给出基于接口的ATI基础模型定义——直接将数据样本x与任务t映射为标签空间上的预测；3）借助"通用任务归约"将模型分解为编码器、模型、解码器三部分；4）据此将误差分解为"设计项"（由任务归约结构决定的可达最优性性质）与"实现模型超额项"（学习组件与程序化组件交互）；5）归纳生成式与判别式两类原型方案。

Result: 建立可操作的ATI形式化定义与误差分解体系，并通过对5个差异显著的真实基础模型案例的分析，验证定义的适用性。

Conclusion: 统一的归约视角使ATI基础模型的结构、误差来源与实现路径可被系统分析；设计项决定结构层面的最优性上限，实现超额项刻画具体系统交互误差，生成式与判别式原型为跨领域落地提供指导。

Abstract: In this work, we provide a formal framework and mathematical definition for the subset of foundation models focused on enabling the property of "any-task inference" (ATI) (similar to zero-shot inference), rather than those designed for pre-training fine-tuning. To achieve this mathematical definition, we formalize the notion of a "space of tasks of interest," which we include in the problem domain, then provide an interface-based formal definition for ATI foundation models as those that map samples of data $x$ and tasks $t$ to predictions over the space of labels directly. While simple, this definition allows us to show that all ATI foundation models can be decomposed through a "universal task reduction" lens into encoder, model, and decoder aspects. We can leverage this decomposition to also decompose foundation model error into terms corresponding to different aspects of this reduction, stratified into a collection of "design terms," which are theoretical properties that can inform one of whether a particular proposed ATI foundation model can achieve optimality or not due to the structure of the proposed task reduction, and "realized model excess" terms which capture interactions between learned and programmatic components of foundation model systems. Lastly, we can use this perspective to offer two "prototypical" recipes for realizing ATI foundation models in diverse domains, which we term generative and discriminative variants of ATI foundation models. This exercise is grounded throughout by 5 real-world examples of foundation models in different settings, varying greatly in how these capabilities are realized, showing the applicability of this definition to real-world settings.

</details>


### [133] [Fair Recommender Systems for Intersecting Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40936)
*Christine Ling,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对含交叉人口群体的动态推荐系统，证明群体盲策略（忽略用户群体信息）在K≥1时会产生线性总遗憾，并提出基于UCB的三种群体感知算法及其实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向平均用户优化的推荐系统难以刻画多元人口群体的偏好；且现实中群体可交叉（一个用户同属多个群体），需要研究在此结构下能否实现次线性遗憾、以及如何设计群体感知推荐策略。

Method: 先理论分析群体盲策略的遗憾下界；再提出三种基于UCB的群体感知算法：i) Intersection-Fitting——在每个交叉群体上独立运行UCB；ii) 两个群体级启发式 Inner-Outer UCB 与 Group-Item UCB——将不同臂分配给不同群体，并用sleeping expert元学习器决定某交叉群体在每步应继承哪个活跃群体的推荐。实验在Goodreads与BoardGameGeek两个数据集上进行。
