Method: 提出十项desiderata作为设计与评估框架；用其对一组代表性现有方法进行对照分析；同时设计一个紧凑的持久世界（persistent-world）场景作为可交互companion，并勾画一个在单次持续生命周期内综合考察多项相关行为能力的基准方案。

Result: 分析表明：现有代表性基准各自只探测目标的不同局部，分散的评测无法证明相应性质在同一智能体的持续生命周期内共存；未发现已发表工作能提供十项要求整合的证据。

Conclusion: 终身持续学习的进步应以十项desiderata的整合为准绳；未来需借助持久世界场景与统一基准，在单一智能体的完整生命周期中联合检验多方面行为能力，而非依赖割裂的单项评测。

Abstract: This paper presents an opinionated set of ten desiderata intended to inform the design and evaluation of reinforcement-learning agents that learn continually throughout a continuing lifetime. We use these desiderata to examine a representative selection of existing approaches. Progress on individual desiderata is valuable, but the longer-term aim should be to integrate all ten within a single agent. To our knowledge, no published evaluation has yet provided evidence for such an integration. Likewise, the representative benchmarks we review probe different parts of this target, but separate evaluations do not establish that the corresponding properties coexist within one agent throughout a continuing lifetime. As a secondary contribution, we therefore present a compact persistent-world scenario as a playable companion to the desiderata and outline a proposed benchmark that examines a broad range of associated behavioral capabilities together within one such lifetime.

</details>


### [204] [DIFFNAS: Predictor-Free Neural Architecture Search via Diffusion Model and Zero-Cost Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41818)
*Yize Sun,Hanzhang Ma,Zixin Wu,Xuechun Li,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DIFFNAS：无需性能预测器，以零成本代理指标（MeCo、SWAP）引导离散图扩散模型进行NAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS依赖模型训练或性能预测器，计算代价高；已有扩散式NAS仍需学习预测器。

Method: 无预测器的生成式NAS框架：用离散图扩散模型生成架构，以训练无关的零成本指标（MeCo、SWAP）作为条件引导生成过程。

Result: NAS-Bench-201上为CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100找到最优架构（94.37%/73.51%），搜索成本较DiffusionNAG最高降17.5倍；MobileNetV3搜索空间上精度低于DiffusionNAG。

Conclusion: 扩散模型结合零成本代理有望实现可扩展、高效的架构搜索，但代理指标在其他搜索空间上的可靠性仍待提升。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has advanced the automated design of deep networks, but remains computationally expensive due to reliance on model training or performance predictors. We introduce DIFFNAS, a predictor-free NAS framework that leverages a discrete graph diffusion model guided by zero-cost performance proxies. Unlike prior diffusion-based NAS methods that require learned performance predictors, DIFFNAS conditions the generative process on training-free metrics such as MeCo and SWAP, enabling efficient architecture search with minimal computational overhead. Experiments on NAS-Bench-201 show that DIFFNAS finds the optimal architectures for CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 (94.37% and 73.51%) without a pretrained predictor, while reducing search cost by up to 17.5x compared to DiffusionNAG. On the MobileNetV3 search space, its accuracy is lower than that of DiffusionNAG. These results highlight the promise of diffusion models combined with zero-cost proxies for scalable and effective architecture search.

</details>


### [205] [ElTab: Elastically Looped Tabular Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41819)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ElTab（弹性循环表格Transformer），通过在随机采样的早期出口处施加监督，使单一模型可在不同计算预算下运行，兼顾表格预测性能与部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN等表格Transformer预测性能强但推理成本高，难以在资源受限环境部署；循环（looped）Transformer虽通过权重共享降低参数量，但过早停止会显著损失预测能力。

Method: Elastically Looped Tabular Transformer：基于循环/权重共享结构，在训练时于随机采样的不同深度早期出口（early exits）同时施加监督，从而让一个模型覆盖多种计算预算（弹性深度训练）。

Result: 在30个OpenML分类数据集上，单个ElTab模型达到与六个深度专用循环模型相当的性能，同时训练成本降低一半以上；在与12层TabPFN式基线性能相当的情况下，推理FLOPs减少25%。

Conclusion: 弹性深度训练可产出单一模型，按部署端实际预算自适应选择计算量，在预测性能、存储与计算成本之间取得良好折中。

Abstract: Tabular transformer models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur substantial inference costs, making them difficult to deploy in resource-constrained settings. While looped transformers reduce parameter count through weight sharing, they can lose significant predictive power when stopped early. To address this challenge, we introduce the Elastically Looped Tabular Transformer (ElTab), a single model trained to operate across different computational budgets through supervision at randomly sampled early exits. Across 30 OpenML classification datasets, a single ElTab model matches six depth-specific looped models while cutting training cost by more than half, and matches a 12-layer TabPFN-style baseline with 25% fewer inference FLOPs. These results show that elastic-depth training yields a single model that adapts to the deployment budget at hand, offering a favorable trade-off among predictive performance, storage, and computational cost.

</details>


### [206] [Trainable Vector Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41822)
*Anastasiia Prutianova,Alina Bogdanova,Mikhail Shutov,Dmitrii Poimanov,Anatoly Gorkov,Ilia Zharikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TVQ 利用直通估计（STE）使向量量化 LLM 可通过梯度下降优化，实现任意目标最小化，在分块知识蒸馏框架下取得低比特 PTQ 的 SOTA 质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQ 可压缩 LLM 权重、降低显存并加速推理，但极端压缩会损害性能；VQ 参数的离散性使性能难以恢复，而现有方法均为免训练方法，仅做逐层重构损失最小化，优化目标受限。

Method: 提出 TVQ：借助直通估计（STE）实现基于梯度下降的向量量化 LLM 优化；通过引入可训练隐权重（trainable latent weights）重构 VQ LLM 优化问题，从而支持任意目标最小化。将 TVQ 嵌入分块（block-wise）知识蒸馏范式以平衡质量与训练效率，并提出若干实用加速训练策略；实现为简洁的 PyTorch。

Result: 在现代 LLM 上的实验表明 TVQ 兼容现有 VQ 方案；在低比特后训练量化（PTQ）设置下以适中的训练预算达到 SOTA 质量。例如对 LLaMA2-70B 实现近乎无损的 2-bit 量化，在常用问答基准上仅 1% 精度下降。

Conclusion: TVQ 证明基于 STE 的可训练 VQ LLM 优化可行且高效，突破了免训练逐层重构的局限，为极端压缩下的 LLM 量化提供了可扩展、易实现的方案。

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) is a rapidly evolving technique for compressing Large Language Model (LLM) weights, reducing memory usage, and speeding up inference. However, extreme compression deteriorates model performance, which is challenging to recover due to the discrete nature of VQ parameters. Existing methods employ training-free approaches that are limited to layer-wise reconstruction loss minimization. To address this limitation, we propose TVQ, a novel method that leverages straight-through estimation to enable gradient descent-based optimization of vector-quantized LLMs. By reformulating VQ LLM optimization with trainable latent weights, TVQ enables arbitrary objective minimization. We constitute our approach by applying TVQ in a block-wise knowledge distillation paradigm, which balances quality and training efficiency, and propose several practical solutions to accelerate training. In experiments on modern LLMs, we demonstrate that TVQ is compatible with modern VQ schemes. As a result, TVQ achieves state-of-the-art quality in low-bit post-training quantization settings with a modest training budget and straightforward PyTorch implementation. For instance, TVQ achieves near-lossless 2-bit quantization for LLaMA2-70B with only 1% accuracy drop on popular Question-Answering benchmarks.

</details>


### [207] [On k-nearest-neighbor regression in metric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41896)
*László Györfi,Vladimir G. Pestov,Harro Walk,Roi Weiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究度量空间值特征下k近邻回归的均方相合性，通过"邻居选择密度"刻画近邻重分配概率，给出逼近误差与条件响应方差的分离条件，并削弱Stone支配条件；同时构造反例表明σ有限Nagata维数不足以保证无界响应下的均方相合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-NN回归的相合性经典结果依赖于Stone支配条件，但该条件在一般度量空间、尤其是无界响应情形下是否必要、能否被更弱的正则性（如选择密度、Nagata维数）替代，尚缺乏系统刻画。作者希望在无原子距离分布的度量空间特征框架下，厘清均方相合的充要性要素，并检验维数条件（σ有限Nagata维）在此问题上的充分性。

Method: 对固定特征分布μ（距离分布无原子），引入独立查询点的"均匀邻居"的密度，用以刻画近邻选择如何重分配概率质量；由此将误差分解为逼近误差项与条件响应方差项，分别给出条件；并以固定总体质量的邻域形式和全局Nagata维数表述相应判据，最后通过构造一个σ有限Nagata维的完备可分空间及一个无噪、各阶矩有限的固定响应函数给出反例。

Result: 当k_n→∞且k_n/n→0时，在响应四阶矩有限的前提下，选择密度的一致L2(μ)界即足以保证均方相合；用固定总体质量的邻域可给出对应条件，并由此恢复欧氏情形下的密度界；全局有限Nagata维数可推出Stone支配条件。但σ有限Nagata维数下，构造出使某一无噪、各阶矩有限响应的均方误差发散的度量空间。

Conclusion: 均方相合性可由选择密度的一致L2界（配合有限四阶矩）保证，是较Stone支配更弱的充分条件；全局Nagata维有限蕴含Stone支配，而σ有限Nagata维虽保证k-NN分类的普遍相合，却不能保证无界响应下k-NN回归的均方相合——分类与回归的相合性在一般度量空间中出现本质分离。

Abstract: We study mean squared consistency of k-nearest-neighbor regression for metric space valued features. For a fixed feature distribution mu with atomless distance distributions, we describe how neighbor selection redistributes probability through the density of a uniformly chosen neighbor of an independent query. This gives separate conditions for the approximation error and the conditional response variance, and weakens Stone's domination condition when stronger response moments are available. In particular, when k_n tends to infinity and k_n/n tends to zero, a uniform L2(mu) bound on the selection densities suffices under a finite fourth response moment. We give a corresponding condition in terms of neighborhoods of fixed population mass and recover the Euclidean density bound. Finite global Nagata dimension gives Stone's domination. While sigma-finite Nagata dimensionality is known to ensure universal consistency of k-NN classification in complete separable spaces, we show that this is not the case for mean squared consistency with unbounded responses: we construct a space in this class on which one fixed noiseless response with all finite moments has diverging mean squared error.

</details>


### [208] [Binary Sparse Codes: Where are we on Energy Efficient Representations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41933)
*Jose Miguel Ramos,Luis SaCouto,Andreas Wichert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出以"相似性保持"为核心的信息保持度量框架，用于定量比较三类（共四种）二值稀疏编码方法，并揭示稀疏性与信息保持之间的普适权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经系统在代谢约束下依靠稀疏皮层表征实现强大计算能力，激发了以稀疏编码为策略的人工系统研究。但现有方法（高效编码理论、反传深度网络、局部Hebbian生物可行方法）的编码质量不仅取决于低激活密度，还取决于输入信息结构的保持，而后者缺乏公认的定量定义，导致无法在原则性基础上比较各方法。

Method: 将"信息保持"形式化为"相似性保持"，提出一组度量指标，量化二值稀疏码在多大程度上复现输入空间的关系几何（relational geometry）；在匹配条件下用该框架对比四种代表性方法：基于高效编码的理论方法、基于反向传播训练的深度学习方法、基于局部Hebbian规则的生物可行方法（含Competitive Sparse Codes）。

Result: 实验显示一致性的权衡：高信息保持的模型需以牺牲稀疏性为代价；而接近最优随机稀疏码均匀性的模型往往损失信息保持。Competitive Sparse Codes（生物可行方法）是唯一同时占据近最大稀疏区间且保留有意义信息保持的架构。

Conclusion: 同时满足高稀疏性与高信息保持仍是该领域的开放挑战；论文将此作为统一评价目标，并提供可复用的评估框架以衡量后续进展。

Abstract: Biological neural systems achieve impressive computational capabilities under tight metabolic constraints, relying on sparse cortical representations that minimise total neural activity. This combination of efficiency and representational power has motivated growing interest in sparse coding as a computational strategy for artificial systems. Artificial models designed to produce binary sparse codes span three broad paradigms, theoretical methods grounded in efficient coding, deep learning methods trained via backpropagation, and biologically plausible methods that learn through local Hebbian rules, each yielding codes whose quality depends not only on achieving low activation density but also on preserving the informational structure of the input. The latter property, however, lacks an agreed-upon quantitative definition, leaving the field without a principled basis for comparing methods. We propose a set of metrics that formalise information preservation as similarity preservation, measuring how faithfully a binary sparse code reproduces the relational geometry of the input space, and apply this framework to compare four representative methods under matched conditions. The results reveal a consistent trade-off in which models that achieve high information preservation do so at the cost of sparsity, while models approaching the uniformity of an optimal random sparse code tend to sacrifice it. Competitive Sparse Codes, a biologically plausible approach, is the only architecture to occupy a region of near-maximal sparsity while retaining meaningful information preservation. These findings frame the simultaneous satisfaction of both factors as an open challenge for the field and provide a reusable evaluation framework for measuring progress toward it.

</details>


### [209] [Observable-Specific Validity Tests for Scientific AI: Controlled Approximations and Intervention Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41939)
*Xiaoshn Nee,Haobo Zhong,Xiaomin Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"可观测特定有效性记录"框架，将近似缩减与其参考模型、误差度量、容差、参数域及变换/干预绑定，并用奇异摄动振子、Hubbard二聚体、神经拟合与因果模型等算例验证其可独立核查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学AI中近似可能复现能量却丢失响应，或满足误差容差却未被给定界认证；需要一种能区分"对哪个可观测量有效"的可核查有效性判据。

Method: 为每个可观测量构造有效性记录（链接缩减、参考模型、误差度量、容差、参数域、变换与干预）；给出奇异摄动振子的位移与速度显式证书；用Hubbard二聚体精确关系分析能量与响应准则接受的耦合域差异；16次重复神经拟合中施加量纲齐次性约束；成对变换与耦合交叉点检验标度关系；因果模型在强负观察关联下恢复正干预响应；对高斯噪声下响应在 $x^k$ 阶首次不同的两机制推导固定控制查询需求的精确标度 $x^{-2k}$；并提供256任务评估接口。

Result: 位移与速度证书给出不同收敛阶并提供充分有效性保证；Hubbard二聚体揭示能量与响应准则接受不同耦合域；量纲齐次性将能量尺度迁移误差从52.3%降至0.0768%，同时满足gap与响应容差的中位比例由0升至100%；成对变换验证受保护的标度关系，耦合交叉识别出响应迁移所需的额外信息；因果模型在强负观察关联下恢复正干预响应；查询需求 $x^{-2k}$ 标度及完整成功曲线经数值验证。

Conclusion: 有效性应针对具体可观测量定义，而非由单一误差或能量匹配隐含推断；该框架配合256任务评估接口，为科学AI中的受控近似、物理迁移与机制判别提供可独立核查的测试。

Abstract: An approximation can reproduce an energy while missing its response, or satisfy an error tolerance without being certified by a given bound. We formulate observable-specific validity records for scientific artificial intelligence (AI) that link a reduction to its reference model, error measure, tolerance, parameter domain, and transformations or interventions. Explicit displacement and velocity certificates for a singularly perturbed oscillator recover distinct convergence orders and provide sufficient validity guarantees. Exact Hubbard dimer relations resolve the different coupling domains accepted by energy and response criteria. Across 16 repeated neural fits, imposing dimensional homogeneity reduces energy scale transfer error from 52.3% to 0.0768% and raises the median fraction jointly satisfying gap and response tolerances from zero to 100%. Paired transformations verify the protected scaling relation, while a coupling crossover identifies the additional information needed for response transfer. A causal model recovers a positive intervention response beneath a strong negative observational association. For two mechanisms observed with Gaussian noise whose responses first differ at order $x^k$, we derive an exact fixed control query requirement scaling as $x^{-2k}$ and verify its full success curve numerically. Together with a 256-task evaluation interface, these results provide independently checkable tests of controlled approximation, physical transfer, and mechanism discrimination in scientific AI.

</details>


### [210] [Chronos-2: A foundation model for multivariate time series forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41956)
*Abdul Fatir Ansari,Oleksandr Shchur,Jaris Küken,Andreas Auer,Boran Han,Pedro Mercado,Syama Sundar Rangapuram,Huibin Shen,Lorenzo Stella,Xiyuan Zhang,Mononito Goswami,Shubham Kapoor,Danielle C. Maddix,Pablo Guerron,Florian Saupe,Markus Reichstein,Tony Hu,Junming Yin,Nick Erickson,Prateek Mutalik Desai,Hao Wang,Huzefa Rangwala,George Karypis,Yuyang Wang,Michael Bohlke-Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出时间序列基础模型 Chronos-2，通过零样本概率预测建模多变量序列间及协变量交互，跨基准平均提升 5.2 个百分点技能分数、10 个百分点胜率，单卡中端 GPU 上 100 条序列预测不足 1 秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数预测模型需针对各领域数据集单独开发与训练，缺乏通用性；已有时序基础模型（TSFM）多将各序列独立处理，无法刻画序列间交互与外部驱动因素，而这类交互在流行病学、经济学、能源、地球科学等科学应用中普遍存在。

Method: Chronos-2 采用大规模预训练的时间序列基础模型，直接由历史上下文推断多变量序列间关系，并统一纳入异质协变量（heterogeneous covariates），无需任务特定处理即可输出零样本概率预测（probabilistic forecasts）。

Result: 在多个基准上取得平均最优精度：技能分数较最强基线提升 5.2 个百分点，胜率提升 10 个百分点；效率方面，单块中端 GPU 上 100 条序列预测耗时不足 1 秒，端到端分别比最佳任务专用点预测器与概率预测器快 50 倍和 1000 倍。在传染病预测、宏观经济建模、碳通量预测、径流模拟等任务中达到或超过领域专用方法。

Conclusion: 单一预训练模型可捕获跨时间系统共享的结构，实现跨领域通用且高效的零样本预测，从而替代按领域分别建模的传统范式。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting with uncertainty quantification is critical in a variety of scientific applications ranging from epidemiology and economics to energy systems and earth sciences. Yet most forecasting models still need to be developed and trained separately for each domain-specific dataset. Time series foundation models (TSFMs) aim to remove this limitation and enable the application of the same model across domains through large-scale pretraining. However, most existing TSFMs treat each time series independently and therefore cannot model interactions among time series and external drivers. Here, we introduce Chronos-2, a foundation model for zero-shot forecasting that models the interactions between multivariate time series and heterogeneous covariates, which commonly arise in scientific applications. The model infers relationships among time series directly from historical context and generates probabilistic forecasts without task-specific treatment. Across various benchmarks, Chronos-2 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on average by improving skill scores by 5.2 percentage points relative to the strongest baselines and increasing win rates by 10 percentage points. Chronos-2 is also computationally efficient, generating forecasts for 100 time series in under one second on a single mid-range GPU, 50x and 1000x faster end-to-end than the best task-specific point and probabilistic forecasters, respectively. In addition, we demonstrate its effectiveness in diverse scientific settings -- including infectious-disease forecasting, macroeconomic modeling, carbon-flux prediction, and runoff simulation -- where the model matches or surpasses specialized methods developed for each domain. These results show that a single pretrained model can capture structure shared across temporal systems.

</details>


### [211] [Robust Learning of Multi-Index Models under Logconcave Distributions via Weak Partial Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41961)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Daniel M. Kane,Mingchen Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向对数凹分布下布尔概念类学习的统一算法框架，基于弱部分学习器的boosting，快速稳健地学习多指标模型、函数之半空间与多类线性分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典boosting需要分布无关的弱学习器（往往难以实现）或只能给出弱近似保证；同时需在对数凹边缘分布下高效处理条件分布失去对数凹性的问题。

Method: 以允许标签部分未定义的"弱部分学习器"实现强不可知学习；设计递归的矩匹配划分：将X与条件分布X'的空间划分为多项式多个凸区域，使多数区域上低阶矩近似一致；在每个好区域上用低阶多项式拟合多指标模型得到弱假设。核心工具是矩张量谱分析与对数凹测度等周不等式结合的矩差异检测，可定位捕捉差异的低维子空间。

Result: 获得比以往显著更快的稳健学习器，并推广至部分分类与可测试学习；在半空间函数与多类线性分类器等基本概念类上取得改进的学习算法。

Conclusion: 弱部分学习器足以支撑强不可知学习；矩差异检测是对数凹分布下boosting可行性的关键，且具独立价值。

Abstract: We develop a new algorithmic framework for learning Boolean concept classes under logconcave distributions. Our framework applies to broad families of multi-index models and yields significantly faster robust learners than were previously known in these settings. It extends to partial classification and testable learning. Consequently, we obtain improved learners for several fundamental concept classes, including functions of halfspaces and multiclass linear classifiers, under logconcave marginals.
  Our starting point is a new boosting framework based on weak partial learners. Classical boosting approaches require often-intractable distribution-free weak learners or yield weak approximation guarantees. We show instead that weak learners for partial classification, where labels may be undefined on part of the domain, suffice for strong agnostic learning. The key challenge is that boosting requires a weak learner that succeeds under conditioned distributions that are not guaranteed to be logconcave. To address this, we develop a recursive moment-based partitioning procedure: given a logconcave distribution $X$ and a conditional version $X'$, we partition the space into polynomially many convex regions so that, on most regions, the low-degree moments of $X$ and $X'$ approximately agree. On each good region, a well-behaved multi-index model correlates with a low-degree polynomial, giving a weak hypothesis. Combining these hypotheses yields the required weak learner.
  A key ingredient of our approach is an efficient method for detecting moment discrepancy. Specifically, we show how to find a low-dimensional subspace that captures any non-trivial discrepancy between $X$ and $X'$, if one exists. Our approach combines spectral analysis of moment tensors with recent progress on isoperimetry for logconcave measures. This discrepancy-finding procedure may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [212] [New Lower Bounds for Private PAC Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41980)
*Prathamesh Dharangutte,Amartya Sanyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究近似差分隐私下的可实现PAC学习，提出独立类打包（ICP）参数作为证明样本复杂度下界的通用框架，并证明VC维与Littlestone维不能决定私有样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VC维和Littlestone维是刻画非私有PAC学习样本复杂度的核心量，但在近似差分隐私（(ε,δ)-DP）下是否能决定可实现的私有样本复杂度尚无定论，尤其对非正常（improper）学习器而言。作者希望厘清这些组合维度与私有样本复杂度之间的关系，并建立统一的低界证明工具。

Method: 引入独立类打包（Independent Class Packing, ICP）参数，定义为可将一族类G独立打包进类H的最大总样本复杂度，据此证明任意(ε,δ)-DP、(α,β)-PAC学习器（允许非正常学习）所需样本数下界为Ω(ICP(H,G)/(αε))；并构造具有相同VC维和Littlestone维但不同私有样本复杂度的概念类作为分离示例。

Result: 对轴对齐矩形、区间并及线性子空间给出紧的低界；对半空间和凸多边形给出改进的低界；对乘积类以及同一族类既可打包进H又能覆盖H定义域（从而可直接利用目标标签）的情形，证明ICP在上至对数因子的意义下刻画了私有PAC样本复杂度。

Conclusion: VC维与Littlestone维不足以决定差分隐私可实现PAC学习的样本复杂度，即使对非正常学习器亦然；ICP参数提供了一套统一且往往紧的低界框架，并在乘积类等结构良好的情形下给出近乎充分的刻画。

Abstract: We study realizable PAC learning under approximate differential privacy, and give a framework for proving lower bounds on its sample complexity. We construct classes with identical VC and Littlestone dimensions but different private sample complexities at common privacy and accuracy parameters, showing that these dimensions do not determine the sample complexity, even for improper learners. To address this we introduce the Independent Class Packing (ICP) parameter, the largest total sample complexity of classes in $\mathcal{G}$ that can be packed independently into a class $\mathcal{H}$, and prove that every $(\varepsilon, δ)$-DP, $(α, β)$-PAC learner for $\mathcal{H}$, possibly improper, uses $Ω\left(\mathrm{ICP}(\mathcal{H}, \mathcal{G})/(α\varepsilon)\right)$ examples. Using this framework we prove tight lower bounds for axis-aligned rectangles, union of intervals and linear subspaces and improved lower bounds for halfspaces and convex polygons. Finally we also show that for product classes, and more generally when the same classes pack into $\mathcal{H}$ and cover its domain so that target labels can be used directly, ICP characterizes private PAC sample complexity up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [213] [Less Loss, Fixed Weights: Logit Corrections for Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42000)
*Hanyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 固定词表偏置加温度的简单校正只能恢复微调/继续预训练造成的部分语言建模能力损失，大部分遗忘无法用受限拟合弥补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在指令微调或领域继续预训练后，对标准网页文本的预测能力下降（"遗忘"）。作者追问：这种损失能否通过仅对最终token预测做简单的固定调整（词表偏置、温度）来逆转。

Method: 在14组基座-衍生模型对（1B–7B参数）上，为每个token拟合固定加分或惩罚（词表偏置），单独使用或与温度联合；在未参与校正拟合的留出文本上评估恢复效果；并设置随机损伤的受控模拟作为对照（无植入偏置或温度变化）。

Result: 留出文本上，最大top-4K偏置恢复估计不足10%（在保留词表条件下计算损失）；偏置加温度最多恢复36%。数学领域继续预训练的模型损失最大且几乎无法恢复。随机损伤模拟中，偏置加温度仍能恢复29%。

Conclusion: 真实模型的大部分损失增长超出受限拟合的可及范围，说明遗忘并非主要源于输出层的简单词表偏置或温度偏移，简单后处理校正作用有限。

Abstract: When a language model is fine-tuned to follow instructions, or further pretrained on a new subject, it often gets worse at predicting standard web text. This is known as "forgetting." We ask whether this loss can be undone by simple, fixed adjustments to the model's final token predictions. Across 14 pairs of base and derived models, from 1B to 7B parameters, we fitted a fixed bonus or penalty for each token (a vocabulary bias), alone or together with a temperature. On text held out from correction fitting, the largest top-4K bias recovery estimate is under 10%, with loss conditional on the retained vocabulary. Combining the bias with a temperature recovers up to 36%. Models further pretrained on math lose the most and recover almost nothing. Finally, in a controlled simulation where a model is damaged at random, with no planted bias or temperature change, the bias-plus-temperature fix still recovers 29%. Most of the real-model loss increase stays out of reach of our restricted fits.

</details>


### [214] [When Does Jacobian Fine-Tuning Stabilize Neural PDE Emulators?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42013)
*Shreeyam Bangera,Manya Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将混沌PDE神经替代模型的误差分解为一步预测误差与模型自身动力学的放大两部分，提出"放大比"指标；据此解释并预测Jacobian微调的收益大小与适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经替代模型以单步预测训练却在自回归中部署，误差会累积；混沌系统中部分误差增长是物理固有的、无法被训练消除，因此需要区分"可训练的误差"与"不可约的动力学放大"，以判断Jacobian类正则化何时真正有效。

Method: 用一阶误差分解，将模型误差与"把相同单步误差经求解器Jacobian传播"的反事实进行对比，定义放大比（模型自身动力学放大其误差的倍数）。实验覆盖4个PDE、5种架构、2种模型规模（8–10k与33k参数）共21种配置，误差在一个Lyapunov时间处测量；比较Jacobian微调与沿模型自身误差方向匹配求解器Jacobian（EDJM），并与随机方向及输入噪声注入对照。

Result: 在34个随机种子中，仅放大比≤1.25的1个种子经Jacobian微调误差下降超10%；而放大比≥4的20个种子全部受益，几何平均误差分别降至1/8（小模型）和1/55（33k模型）。增益约等于微调的一步精度代价除以放大比。EDJM在35个种子中的28个优于同成本的随机方向；输入噪声注入效果与EDJM相当。

Conclusion: 误差放大比是预测Jacobian微调收益的关键可诊断量：高放大比时收益巨大，低放大比时收益甚微。这为在混沌PDE替代模型中按放大比选择性使用Jacobian正则化提供了依据。

Abstract: Neural emulators of chaotic PDEs are trained on one-step prediction but deployed autoregressively, where their errors compound. Jacobian-based regularisers aim to curb this growth, but in a chaotic system part of the growth is physical and cannot be trained away. We separate the two sources of this error with a first-order decomposition. We compare the emulator's error with a counterfactual that propagates the same one-step mistakes through the solver's Jacobian, giving an amplification ratio: the factor by which the model's own dynamics amplify its errors. We study 21 configurations across four PDEs, five architectures and two model sizes (8-10k and 33k parameters), with errors measured at one Lyapunov time. Jacobian fine-tuning reduced the error by more than 10% for 1 of 34 seeds whose amplification ratio was at most 1.25, and for all 20 seeds whose ratio was at least four; for the latter, the error fell by a factor of 8 (small models) and 55 (33k models) in geometric mean over seeds. The gain approximately equals the one-step accuracy cost of fine-tuning divided by the amplification ratio. Matching the solver's Jacobian along the model's own error direction (EDJM) outperformed random directions at the same cost on 28 of 35 seeds; input-noise injection performed comparably to EDJM.

</details>


### [215] [Know When the Equation Is Wrong: Structural Uncertainty for LLM-Guided Symbolic Regression under Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42024)
*Mostafa Cham,Bayu Adhi Tama,Jianwu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM-SR-UQ 通过保留多个竞争方程结构并利用其 disagreement（结构性区间）来刻画函数形式不确定性，改善符号回归在分布外外推时的区间覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 引导的符号回归方法多只输出单个最优方程，缺乏对备选函数形式的不确定性量化；不同方程可同样拟合观测数据却外推差异巨大，而标准 conformal 区间虽在分布内达到名义覆盖，在外推下经常失效。

Method: 保留多个竞争方程结构，以其预测 disagreement 表征函数形式层面的不确定性；将标准固定宽度 conformal 方法扩展为"结构性区间"（structural intervals），度量候选方程间分歧并识别预测区间应加宽的位置。

Result: 在 LLM-SRBench 上，标准 conformal 区间分布内覆盖达标但外推下频繁失败；所提框架通过引入结构 disagreement 显著提升覆盖达成率，同时保持分布内覆盖；应用于其他符号回归方法时也表现出良好泛化性。

Conclusion: 以多方程结构 disagreement 为核心的 UQ 框架能有效弥补固定宽度 conformal 在外推下的覆盖不足，且具方法可移植性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can help discover mathematical equations from data, but most LLM-guided symbolic regression methods focus on a single best equation without quantifying uncertainty over alternative functional forms. This is challenging because distinct equations can fit the observed data similarly while producing substantially different extrapolations. Our experiments on the LLM-SRBench dataset show that standard conformal intervals achieve nominal in-distribution coverage but frequently fail under extrapolation. To tackle this challenge, we introduce LLM-SR-UQ, a framework that retains competing equation structures and uses their disagreement to characterize uncertainty about the underlying functional form. We extend the standard fixed-width conformal approach to quantify structural intervals, which measure disagreement among candidate equations and identify where prediction intervals should widen. Extensive experiments show our framework substantially improves coverage attainment by incorporating structural disagreement while preserving in-distribution coverage. Our approach also shows good generalizability when it is applied to other symbolic-regression methods.

</details>


### [216] [Hear No Evil: Detecting Gradient Leakage by Malicious Servers in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20651)
*Fei Wang,Baochun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从防御者视角首次系统分析恶意梯度泄露攻击及其模型操纵手段，指出攻击在"重构效果"与"隐蔽性"之间存在本质权衡，在含归一化与联邦平均的真实FL设置下难以兼得，因此实践中受限且可被基础监控检测；并附带提出一种轻量的客户端侧预训练检测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明联邦学习中的梯度更新会无意泄露客户端本地敏感信息，而恶意服务器通过操纵全局模型可诱发信息量更大的更新，使风险显著上升；但缺乏对该类攻击及模型操纵技术的系统性防御视角分析。

Method: 以防御者为出发点，全面分析恶意梯度泄露攻击及支撑其的模型操纵技术，并在包含常见归一化技术与联邦平均的真实FL设置下考察攻击表现；基于分析结论提出一种简单、轻量、普适的客户端侧检测机制，在本地训练开始前标记可疑的模型更新。

Result: 揭示核心权衡：攻击无法同时实现高重构效果与足够隐蔽以规避检测，尤其在含归一化与联邦平均的现实FL设置下；恶意梯度泄露攻击虽理论上值得关注，但实践中本质受限，且常可通过基础监控被发现。

Conclusion: 恶意梯度泄露攻击的实际威胁有限，低成本防御可行；所提客户端侧检测机制以极小开销提供可部署的防护手段，为注重隐私的联邦学习系统提供保障。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that gradient updates in federated learning (FL) can unintentionally reveal sensitive information about a client's local data. This risk becomes significantly greater when a malicious server manipulates the global model to provoke information-rich updates from clients. In this paper, we adopt a defender's perspective to provide the first comprehensive analysis of malicious gradient leakage attacks and the model manipulation techniques that enable them. Our investigation reveals a core trade-off: these attacks cannot be both highly effective in reconstructing private data and sufficiently stealthy to evade detection -- especially in realistic FL settings that incorporate common normalization techniques and federated averaging.
  Building on this insight, we argue that malicious gradient leakage attacks, while theoretically concerning, are inherently limited in practice and often detectable through basic monitoring. As a complementary contribution, we propose a simple, lightweight, and broadly applicable client-side detection mechanism that flags suspicious model updates before local training begins, despite the fact that such detection may not be strictly necessary in realistic FL settings. This mechanism further underscores the feasibility of defending against these attacks with minimal overhead, offering a deployable safeguard for privacy-conscious federated learning systems.

</details>


### [217] [GhostPrefix: Synchronization-Free Universal Prefix Attacks for ASR Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42026)
*Shengding Liu,Ashwin Innuganti,Qiben Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GhostPrefix 是首个针对 ASR 系统的免同步通用对抗前缀攻击，可学习一段短小可复用的音频片段前置到任意实时语音，实现实时操控识别输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有音频对抗攻击多为逐句定制、需与受害者波形同步、或目标狭窄；作者希望实现无需预知未来语音内容与时间对齐的实时通用控制。

Method: 核心挑战是跨未知、演化语音上下文保持稳定对抗效果。方法结合令牌级损失与新颖的表示级目标以获得输入无关的稳定效果；支持三种模式：提示式前缀（语义引导）、定向前缀（转录劫持）、非定向前缀（转录退化）。并通过向良性环境声分布正则化提升感知隐蔽性，增强对空口失真的鲁棒性。

Result: 跨 4 个家族 13 个开源 ASR 模型评估：数字域提示引导平均成功率 97.6%、定向劫持 93.4%，非定向攻击 WER 达 1.18；空口条件下两模式成功率分别为 91.5% 和 89.5%。这些操控可传播至语音助手、AI 笔记等下游流水线，造成系统性语义误读。

Conclusion: GhostPrefix 揭示了免同步通用前缀是现实 ASR 及下游应用中一种实用且尚未充分探索的威胁。

Abstract: We present GhostPrefix, the first synchronization-free universal adversarial prefix attack against automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems. Unlike prior audio attacks, which are typically crafted per utterance, require synchronization with the victim waveform, or target narrow objectives, GhostPrefix learns a short, reusable audio segment prepended to arbitrary live speech to exert real-time control over ASR outputs without knowledge of the future utterance or temporal alignment. A key challenge is maintaining stable adversarial effects across unknown, evolving speech contexts. GhostPrefix combines token-level losses with a novel representation-level objective for stable, input-agnostic effects. GhostPrefix supports three attack modes: (i) prompted prefix attacks for semantic steering, (ii) targeted prefix attacks for transcript hijacking, and (iii) untargeted prefix attacks for transcription degradation. To remain perceptually inconspicuous, we regularize prefixes toward benign environmental-sound distributions and improve robustness against over-the-air distortions. We evaluate GhostPrefix across 13 open-source ASR models from four families. In digital settings, it achieves average attack success rates of 97.6% for prompted steering and 93.4% for targeted hijacking, while driving word error rate to 1.18 under untargeted attacks. Over the air, it achieves success rates of 91.5% and 89.5% for prompted and targeted modes, respectively. These manipulations propagate into downstream AI-assisted pipelines, including voice assistants and AI note-taking tools, causing systematic semantic misinterpretations. GhostPrefix exposes synchronization-free universal prefixes as a practical, under-explored threat to real-world ASR and downstream applications. Artifacts and demos are available at https://ghostprefix.github.io/.

</details>


### [218] [Leaner Training, Lower Leakage: Revisiting Memorization in LLM Fine-Tuning with LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20856)
*Fei Wang,Baochun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视微调中的记忆化问题，发现LoRA微调在记忆化规律上显著不同于预训练与全参数微调：模型规模、数据重复等因素不再遵循相同趋势；在更宽松的基于相似度的记忆化度量下，LoRA相比全参数微调大幅降低记忆化风险，同时保持强劲的任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的记忆化使其面临数据抽取攻击风险。预训练阶段的记忆化已被广泛研究，但微调阶段（尤其是广泛采用的LoRA参数高效微调）中的记忆化影响研究较少，亟需系统性考察不同微调策略下记忆化规律的差异。

Method: 重新考察微调中的记忆化现象，对比LoRA微调与全参数微调在不同因素（模型规模、数据重复等）下的记忆化表现；并采用更宽松的基于相似度的记忆化度量指标进行评估，同时考察任务性能。

Result: 在预训练与全参数微调中强烈影响记忆化的因素（模型规模、数据重复）在LoRA微调中并不遵循相同趋势，呈现出与既有发现相悖的分化；基于相似度的度量显示LoRA相比全参数微调显著降低记忆化风险，且仍保持强任务性能。

Conclusion: LoRA微调在记忆化特性上不同于预训练与全参数微调，其记忆化风险显著更低，可作为兼顾隐私安全与任务性能的微调选择，也提示记忆化结论不能简单跨微调策略外推。

Abstract: Memorization in large language models (LLMs) makes them vulnerable to data extraction attacks. While pre-training memorization has been extensively studied, fewer works have explored its impact in fine-tuning, particularly for LoRA fine-tuning, a widely adopted parameter-efficient method.
  In this work, we re-examine memorization in fine-tuning and uncover a surprising divergence from prior findings across different fine-tuning strategies. Factors such as model scale and data duplication, which strongly influence memorization in pre-training and full fine-tuning, do not follow the same trend in LoRA fine-tuning. Using a more relaxed similarity-based memorization metric, we demonstrate that LoRA significantly reduces memorization risks compared to full fine-tuning, while still maintaining strong task performance.

</details>


### [219] [CausalXRL: Explainable Reinforcement Learning through Causal Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42028)
*Yanming Zhang,Eric Papenhausen,Klaus Mueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CausalXRL——一个基于因果图推理的可解释强化学习（XRL）框架，能够提供透明、结构化、多层次的智能体决策解释，并兼顾解释保真度与因果结构学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在复杂环境中表现优异，但以可解释性为代价，导致信任度下降、行为调试与改进困难，因此需要可解释强化学习方法。

Method: CausalXRL 采用因果图推理（causal graph reasoning），对智能体决策生成透明、结构化、多层次的解释。验证方式包括案例研究，以及两部分评估：(1) 在基准 RL 环境中定量分析解释保真度与因果结构学习效率；(2) 在 RTS 基准 MicroRTS 上开展专家定性研究评估可解释性。

Result: 定量结果表明 CausalXRL 在高效学习因果结构的同时能给出保真解释；定性专家研究显示参与者认为该框架有助于审视 RTS 高层策略。

Conclusion: CausalXRL 通过因果图推理为强化学习智能体提供多层次可靠解释，在解释保真度与结构学习效率上表现良好，并在真实 RTS 场景中获得专家认可，具备实用价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a powerful paradigm for training autonomous agents and has achieved impressive performance in complex environments. However, this success often comes at the cost of interpretability, diminishing trust and complicating efforts to debug and improve agent behavior. To address these challenges, we introduce CausalXRL, a novel framework for explainable reinforcement learning (XRL). A key feature of CausalXRL is its use of causal graph reasoning, which provides transparent, structured, multi-level explanations of agent decision-making. We validate CausalXRL through comprehensive case studies and a two-part evaluation: (1) a quantitative analysis of explanation fidelity and causal-structure learning efficiency in benchmark RL environments, and (2) a qualitative expert study assessing explainability in the real-time strategy (RTS) benchmark MicroRTS. The quantitative results show that CausalXRL can provide faithful explanations while efficiently learning causal structures, and the qualitative expert study suggests that participants found CausalXRL useful for inspecting high-level RTS strategies.

</details>


### [220] [There Is No Free Loop: When Does Looping a Trained Model Pay for Its Inference Compute?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42030)
*Rhea Sanjay Sukthanker,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文检验"在已预训练模型上加循环（looping）"是否划算：在Qwen3/Qwen3.5 0.8B–9B上，只有0.8B的循环模型能同时胜过等参数与等FLOPs两个基线；4B和9B上循环模型甚至不如同规模无循环模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环复用层块可在不增加参数量、磁盘体积、显存和训练成本的前提下提升模型，但每生成一个token的成本会变为约(N+m)/N倍。大规模预训练中学到的循环能证明这一代价合理，但"预训练之后再追加循环"是否值得尚不清楚，本文旨在澄清这一点。

Method: 在同一预训练模型上追加对N层中m层的循环，并与两个简单基线对比：(1) 相同参数量的无循环模型（同等训练）；(2) 在相同推理FLOPs下最优的无循环配置。实验覆盖Qwen3与Qwen3.5，参数量0.8B至9B。

Result: 仅循环版0.8B模型同时胜过两个基线；在4B和9B上，循环模型未能胜过第一个基线（即同等训练的无循环同款模型）。该结论并非因基准过于简单——同一基准上无循环9B模型比4B模型高出最多13分，说明仍有充足提升空间。

Conclusion: 在已预训练模型中引入循环，仅在"参数量预算固定的小模型"场景下才划得来；对中大规模模型（4B、9B）而言，预训练后加循环的收益不足以抵消其推理开销。

Abstract: Looping re-applies a block of layers so that each token passes through the block more than once. Adding a loop to a trained model is attractive because it leaves the parameter count, and hence the disk size, the memory footprint and the training cost unchanged. Unfortunately, every generated token pays for the loop. Re-applying m of N layers makes each token about (N+m)/N times as expensive. Loops that a model learns during large-scale pretraining justify this price, but whether loops added after pretraining justify this cost remains unclear. We compare a looped model with two simple baselines. We first compare it with the same model trained identically without the loop (equal parameters). Secondly, we compare it with the best unlooped use of the same inference floating-point operations (equal FLOPs). Across Qwen3 and Qwen3.5 models from 0.8B to 9B parameters, only the looped 0.8B model beats both baselines. At 4B and 9B, the looped model does not beat the first baseline, i.e. the same model trained without the loop. This is not because the benchmarks are too easy, since on the same benchmarks the unlooped 9B model still scores up to 13 points higher than the 4B model, showing that there is ample room for improvement. Introducing loops in an already pretrained model therefore only pays for small models with a fixed parameter budget.

</details>


### [221] [A richness-sensitive Acoustic Biodiversity Index from species-level detections: classifier benchmarking, false-positive control and validation against standard soundscape indices in an African savanna and an urban zoo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42042)
*Muhammad Saeed,Dmitry Mikhaylov,Mohamed H. Alhosani,Yasser F. Al Wahedi,Rory Pilossof,Ahmed Arabi,Walid Shaaba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出并检验基于物种级检测的声学生物多样性指数ABI，以Shannon多样性按区域物种库归一化(ABI_E)，辅以时间稳定性T；在津巴布韦稀树草原与阿布扎比动物园录音中，ABI_E与物种丰富度强相关(rho=0.93)，显著优于6种传统声学指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声学指数(summarise声能分布)反映能量而非发声物种，对物种丰富度追踪能力差，需构建可解释的物种级生物多样性指标。

Method: 1) 在强标注热带数据集(1,216条鸣声、24类)上以录音分组交叉验证基准测试4个分类器，BirdNET嵌入+逻辑回归最优(macro-F1=0.750±0.029)，并揭示随机帧级划分导致准确率虚高(0.41→0.70)；2) 使用BirdNET v2.4叠加地点与季节过滤，应用于津巴布韦赞比西河谷稀树草原及阿布扎比动物园三生境录音；3) 严格事后地点过滤剔除动物园9个不合理物种的517条高置信检测；4) 按昼夜节律匹配的稀释法统一至6小时等努力量；5) 定义ABI_E=H/ln S_ref，T作为独立分量，并与6种标准指数对比。

Result: 等努力量(6 h)下稀树草原21种(1D=9.83, ABI_E=0.38)，动物园1.3–13种(ABI_E=0.03–0.21)；149小时录音中ABI_E与丰富度强相关(Spearman rho=0.93)，6种标准指数均未达此水平(最佳ACI, rho=0.48)；稳定性T在稀树草原更低(0.39 vs 0.65–0.67)，表明其惩罚自然昼夜活动节律。

Conclusion: 具备显式假阳性控制、声明式区域参考物种库与努力量标准化的物种级指数，比能量型声学指数更具可解释性与生态指示价值；但T分量需谨慎解读，因其混淆自然节律。

Abstract: Acoustic indices are widely used as rapid proxies for biodiversity, but they summarise the distribution of sound energy rather than the species producing it and often track richness poorly. We revise and test an Acoustic Biodiversity Index (ABI) built from species-level detections. Its primary form, ABI_E, is Shannon diversity of detections normalised by the regional species pool expected at the site and season (H / ln S_ref); temporal stability T is reported as a separate component. We first benchmarked four classifiers on a strongly labelled tropical dataset (1,216 calls, 24 classes) with recording-grouped cross-validation. BirdNET embeddings + logistic regression performed best (macro-F1 0.750 +/- 0.029), and random frame-level splits inflated accuracy from 0.41 to 0.70. We then applied BirdNET v2.4 with a location and season filter to recordings from a riverine savanna in the Zambezi Valley, Zimbabwe, and from three habitats at Emirates Park Zoo and Resort, Abu Dhabi, United Arab Emirates. A stricter post-hoc location filter removed 517 high-confidence detections of 9 implausible species, all at the zoo. After diel-matched rarefaction to equal effort (6 h), the savanna site held 21 species (Hill number 1D = 9.83; ABI_E = 0.38) against 1.3-13 species (ABI_E = 0.03-0.21) at the zoo sites. Across 149 recorded hours, ABI_E correlated strongly with richness (Spearman rho = 0.93), whereas six standard indices did not (best: ACI, rho = 0.48). The stability term was lower at the savanna site (0.39) than at the zoo (0.65-0.67), showing that it penalises the natural diel cycle of bird activity. Species-level indices with explicit false-positive control, a declared regional reference pool and effort standardisation provide a more interpretable biodiversity indicator than energy-based acoustic indices.

</details>


### [222] [Differentially Private Synthetic Data from Sliced Chebyshev Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42081)
*Lucas Rosenblatt,R. Teal Witter,Christopher Musco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出差分隐私合成数据算法SPLICE，通过匹配噪声切片Chebyshev多项式矩，在保持实用SOTA性能的同时获得与维度d无关的全局分布保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实用DP合成数据方法主要近似保留低维边际，全局结构只能"碰运气"保留；而追求全局精度保证的方法代价随维度d指数增长，受困于维度灾难。

Method: 测量目标分布的噪声切片Chebyshev多项式矩，并拟合分布使其匹配（借鉴一维多项式合成数据方法的变体）；实现上借用张量分解工具。

Result: 证明在常数隐私参数下，给定n条样本，SPLICE返回的私有分布与目标的Sliced Wasserstein-1距离为Õ(1/√n)，且该界不依赖维度d；另证一独立有意义结论：单位球分布沿Õ(d/α²)个随机方向匹配前O(1/α)个Chebyshev矩即可保证α的Sliced Wasserstein误差；大量基准上性能优于已有DP合成数据方法。

Conclusion: SPLICE在高维下同时实现与维度无关的全局分布保证和实用SOTA精度，为DP合成数据的维度灾难提供可行应对。

Abstract: Research on practical differentially private (DP) synthetic data has largely focused on methods that approximately preserve low-dimensional marginals of a target distribution, and can thus only hope to preserve higher-dimensional, global structure as a lucky by-product. At the same time, existing methods that target global accuracy guarantees pay a price exponential in the data dimension, $d$.
  In this work, we address this ``curse of dimensionality'' by presenting a new DP synthetic data algorithm, SPLICE, that comes with a compelling global distributional guarantee, even in high-dimensions, and moreover, is practically state-of-the-art. Our algorithm is based on measuring noisy sliced Chebyshev polynomial moments of the target distribution and fitting a distribution to match, a twist on recent polynomial methods for 1-D synthetic data.
  We prove that, given a dataset of $n$ examples, SPLICE returns a private distribution with Sliced Wasserstein-1 distance $\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$ from the target, for constant privacy parameters. Notably, this bound is independent of the data dimension, $d$. Along the way, we prove a moment matching result of independent interest: for a distribution on the unit ball, matching the first $O(1/α)$ Chebyshev moments along $\tilde{O}(d/α^2)$ random directions guarantees $α$ Sliced Wasserstein error.
  Finally, we provide a practical implementation of the algorithm that borrows tools from the tensor decomposition literature and achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to prior DP synthetic data methods on an extensive benchmark.

</details>


### [223] [Cost-Aware Stopping for Freeze-Thaw Bayesian Optimization: When Does One-Step Lookahead Suffice?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42085)
*Long Vu,Jongseong Brad Choi,Binoy Ravindran,Zhoulai Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"EI stopping"——基于一步前瞻的最大期望改进(EI)与增量成本比较的免预算停止准则，并构造两步前瞻价值下界证书来纠正其过早停止；理论证明该准则不劣于立即停止、纠正重复迭代不降低期望效用，并在超参优化基准上验证成本依赖的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Freeze-thaw 贝叶斯优化以可恢复增量方式评估候选点，但通常停在固定预算或收敛判据处，缺乏"预算无关"的成本感知停止原则。作者希望在"最优观测分数减去每次增量成本"的免预算效用下，明确何时应继续、何时应停止。

Method: 定义免预算效用（best observed score − 每增量成本），采用一步前瞻：固定代理模型下推进 EI 最大的候选，当无候选 EI 超过成本时停止（EI stopping）。进一步构造两步前瞻价值的下界"证书"，用于证明某次停止为时过早，并迭代施加纠正。

Result: 理论上 EI stopping 的期望效用不低于立即停止，且重复纠正不降低代理模型下的期望效用；在代理模型上，低成本时 EI 停止平均而言过早，高成本时损失很小。三个超参优化基准的预注册检验显示：多数高成本设置下 EI stopping 不劣于纠正方法，两个基准在低成本下纠正带来增益；代理模型共享信息更多时 EI stopping 效用更高。在两个已发表方法的代理模型上，EI stopping 以更低分数换取更少训练增量。

Conclusion: EI stopping 是一个简单、成本感知的参考基线，其效用取决于代理模型质量；两步前瞻证书可作为诊断与改进过早停止的工具。

Abstract: Freeze-thaw Bayesian optimization (BO) evaluates candidates in resumable increments but usually stops at a budget or convergence test. We maximize a budget-free utility, the best observed score minus a cost per increment, with a one-step lookahead: advance the candidate with the largest expected improvement (EI) under a fixed surrogate, and stop once no EI exceeds the cost. This rule, EI stopping, never falls below stopping immediately in expected utility, yet it can stop too early. We construct a certificate, a lower bound on the value of two-step lookahead, that proves such a stop premature, and repeating this correction never lowers expected utility under the surrogate. Under the surrogate, EI stops are premature on average at low costs and lose little at high costs. On three hyperparameter optimization benchmarks, a pre-registered test finds EI stopping noninferior to the correction in most high-cost settings, while the correction gains at low costs on two of the three; more information sharing in the surrogate raises EI stopping's utility. On the surrogates of two published methods, EI stopping trades lower scores for fewer training increments. EI stopping is a simple cost-aware reference whose utility depends on the surrogate.

</details>


### [224] [Towards Understanding the Impact of Plasticity Loss on Reinforcement Learning in Stochastic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42100)
*Philipp Bordne,André Biedenkapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了随机环境（观测噪声与动作噪声）下深度强化学习的可塑性损失问题，发现保可塑性方法在较难任务上随观测噪声增大优势扩大，且随机性对可塑性损失的影响依任务难度而异——难任务中加速可塑性丧失，简单任务中噪声反而起正则化作用；在最具挑战性的随机设定下，TD不稳定而非渐进性可塑性损失成为主导失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度RL虽在序贯决策中成功显著，但以"脆弱"著称，新兴研究将其归因于神经网络可塑性损失。然而既有工作主要在确定性环境中展开，随机环境下的可塑性损失研究不足，二者交互机制尚不清楚，故需系统考察。

Method: 在三个DeepMind Control任务上训练SAC智能体，任务分别注入受控水平的观测噪声与动作噪声；对比保可塑性方法（如重初始化、层归一化及其组合）在不同噪声水平下的表现，并分析可塑性损失随训练阶段的演化及失效模式。

Result: (1) 保可塑性方法提升两个较难任务的学习鲁棒性，且优势随观测噪声增大而扩大；(2) 在所有测试噪声水平下，"重初始化+层归一化"组合均劣于单独重初始化；(3) 随机性在较难环境中加速可塑性损失，在较易环境中噪声可充当正则化项维持可塑性；(4) 可塑性损失在训练早期最为显著；(5) 最具挑战性的随机设定下，TD不稳定取代渐进性可塑性损失成为主要失效模式。

Conclusion: 随机环境与可塑性损失的交互具有任务难度依赖性，保可塑性方法的效果并非单调叠加；实践中应区别对待噪声水平与任务难度，并在高随机性场景下优先关注TD学习稳定性，而非仅依赖重初始化等保可塑性手段。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) is a powerful framework for sequential decision-making. Despite many high-profile successes in recent years, deep RL is known to be brittle. An emerging line of research attributes this brittleness to a loss of plasticity in neural networks. However, plasticity loss has so far been understudied in stochastic environments. We study this interplay systematically, training SAC agents on three DeepMind Control tasks augmented with controlled levels of observation and action noise. We find that plasticity-preserving methods increase learning robustness on the two harder tasks, with their advantage growing as observation noise increases, while combining resets with layer normalization underperforms resets alone at every observation noise level tested. Further, stochasticity can accelerate the loss of plasticity in harder environments, whereas in easier settings noise can act as a regularizer that sustains plasticity. Lastly, plasticity loss is most pronounced in the early stages of training. In the most challenging stochastic settings, TD instability rather than gradual plasticity loss becomes the dominant failure mode.

</details>


### [225] [Hyperbolic Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42104)
*Iulia Rusu,Anoop Praturu,Sergei Gepshtein,Tatyana Sharpee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双曲逆强化学习（Hyperbolic IRL），将奖励参数化为特征向量与学习到的参数向量之间的双曲距离（及其欧氏距离变体），在低数据、密集环境和博物馆人类导航任务上优于或媲美基线，并能揭示特征间不可分的合取结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IRL奖励函数（线性权重、神经网络等函数近似器）均未对底层特征空间的几何结构作任何假设，这限制了预测精度与可解释性。神经回路中观察到的几何组织启发了引入双曲几何作为奖励参数化方式的思路。

Method: 提出Hyperbolic IRL：以特征向量与学习参数向量之间的双曲距离作为奖励函数，并构造其欧氏距离对照变体；在确定性策略与随机策略下，与多种线性及非线性IRL基线进行对比测试。

Result: 双曲方法在已有基准上与SOTA模型性能相当，在低数据情形和密集环境中表现突出；在博物馆多展品房间的人类导航数据上，揭示了不仅依赖单个展品距离特征、还依赖特征间不可分合取的奖励结构。

Conclusion: 双曲奖励参数化可同时提升IRL的预测精度与可解释性，为研究不可分、合取式结构如何解释行为提供了框架。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) seeks to infer the reward function underlying observed behavior, but its predictive accuracy and interpretability depend on how that reward is computed from the underlying features. While prior IRL reward functions have been implemented through a variety of techniques including linear weights, neural networks, or other function approximators, none make assumptions about the geometry of the underlying feature space. Inspired by geometric organization observed in neural circuits, we introduce Hyperbolic inverse reinforcement learning (Hyperbolic IRL), which parameterizes reward as a function of Hyperbolic distance between the feature vector and a learned parameter vector, as well as its Euclidean distance variant. We tested our methods against a range of linear and nonlinear IRL baselines under both deterministic and stochastic policies. Our Hyperbolic approach achieved comparable performance to state-of-the-art models on established benchmarks and excelled in the low data limit and across dense environments. Applied to human navigation among multiple exhibits in a museum room, Hyperbolic IRL reveals reward structure that depends not only on individual exhibit-distance features, but also on non-separable conjunctions between them. Together, these results demonstrate that a Hyperbolic reward parameterization can improve both the predictive accuracy and interpretability of IRL, providing a framework for studying how non-separable, conjunctive structure can begin to explain behavior.

</details>


### [226] [Fast Privacy Loss Accounting for Random Allocation via Multiscale FFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42117)
*Vitaly Feldman,Moshe Shenfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对随机分配采样（random allocation sampling）方案的隐私损失分布（PLD）会计器，本文提出 multiscale FFT 算法：在二分区间内均匀网格上表示似然比，并在每个区间内用 FFT 计算所有两两求和的精确舍入，将运行时间对精度参数 α 的依赖从 O(α^{-2}) 降至 O(α^{-1}log(1/α))；同时用均值保持的 connect-the-dots 投影改进舍入误差，将隐私损失均值与 χ² 散度的误差从量级 α 降到 O(α²/log t)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机分配采样在隐私优化与通信高效聚合中优于 Poisson 采样，但其现有 PLD 会计器运行时间为精度参数 α 的二次方级（O(α^{-2})），高精度场景下计算代价过高；同时网格舍入会向隐私损失均值与 χ² 散度引入量级 α 的误差，削弱隐私界紧致性。

Method: 提出 multiscale FFT 算法：将似然比表示在"每个二分（dyadic）区间内均匀"的多尺度网格上，从而把动态范围分解到各尺度；在每个区间内部用 FFT 计算所有两两卷积/求和，并对结果做精确舍入，保证结果与原会计器的严格性；此外证明舍入可通过 Doroshenko 等人（2026）的均值保持 connect-the-dots 投影实现，以抑制舍入偏差。

Result: 在保持原会计器保证的前提下，运行时间对 α 的依赖由 O(α^{-2}) 改进为 O(α^{-1}log(1/α))；舍入在隐私损失均值与 χ² 散度上引入的误差由 O(α) 降至 O(α²/log t)。实验表明在高精度下取得数个数量级的加速，并在结合 PREAMBLE 的 DP-SGD 上给出更紧的隐私界。

Conclusion: 多尺度 FFT 表示与均值保持投影舍入，使随机分配采样方案的 PLD 会计在高精度区间变得实用，兼具更好的时间复杂度与更小的舍入偏差，可直接用于改进 DP-SGD 等实际隐私训练流程的隐私核算。

Abstract: In the random allocation sampling scheme each user's data is used in $k$ steps chosen randomly and uniformly from a sequence of $t$ steps. This scheme is known to improve on Poisson sampling in private optimization and in communication-efficient aggregation. The existing privacy loss distribution (PLD) accountant for this scheme runs in time quadratic in the inverse of the accuracy parameter $α$ (Feldman and Shenfeld, 2026). We give an algorithm, \emph{multiscale FFT}, that represents likelihood ratios on a grid that is uniform within each dyadic interval and computes the exact rounding of every pairwise sum using FFTs within each interval. It keeps the guarantees of the accountant and improves the dependence of its running time on $α$ from $O(α^{-2})$ to $O(α^{-1}\log(1/α))$. In addition, we demonstrate that rounding can be performed via the mean-preserving connect-the-dots projection of (Doroshenko et al., 2026). This reduces the errors that rounding introduces in the mean of the privacy loss and in the $χ^2$ divergence of the computed pair from the order of $α$ to $O(α^2/\log(t))$. In our experiments, our algorithm achieves speedups of several orders of magnitude at high accuracy and gives improved privacy bounds for DP-SGD with PREAMBLE.

</details>


### [227] [Gradient Descent on Two ReLU Neurons: Global Landscape and Bifurcation Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42118)
*Binghua Li,Mengzhe Li,Denny Wu,Tianhao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在最简的两神经元 ReLU 教师-学生多指标模型中，用总体梯度流刻画了由"易"角平分线方向与"难"分裂方向组织的多阶段对称破缺动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解梯度下降如何学习特征仍是神经网络理论的核心难题；作者希望在一个已能展露丰富多阶段动力学的极小可解模型中，分离出特征学习的本质机制。

Method: 采用各向同性高斯输入下的良好设定（well-specified）两神经元 ReLU 教师-学生模型，从小初始化出发运行总体（population）梯度流；先解析总体平方损失景观，证明所有学生权重均非零的临界点只能是全局极小或鞍点，再追踪梯度流轨迹刻画逃逸与特化过程。

Result: 动力学由两个方向组织："易"的角平分线方向承载主导信号，"难"的分裂方向支配特化；轨迹上学生神经元首先坍缩至角平分线上的鞍点，随后逃逸并各自特化到教师神经元。

Conclusion: 该工作从损失景观与动力学两方面解释了简单多指标模型在标准算法与架构选择下出现的多阶段对称破缺行为，为理解特征学习提供了最小可解范式。

Abstract: Understanding how gradient descent learns features remains a central challenge in neural network theory. We study this question in a minimal multi-index setting that already exhibits rich multi-phase gradient dynamics: a well-specified two-neuron ReLU teacher-student model under isotropic Gaussian inputs, trained by population gradient flow from small initialization. We show that the dynamics are organized by two directions: the "easy" bisector direction, which carries the leading signal, and the "hard" splitting direction, which governs specialization. To characterize the loss structure and learning behavior, we analyze the population squared-loss landscape and show that every critical point with both student weights nonzero is either a global minimum or a saddle. We then track the gradient-flow trajectory, showing that student neurons first collapse toward a bisector saddle before escaping and specializing to teacher neurons. Our results provide both a landscape and dynamics account of the multi-phase symmetry-breaking behavior that arises in a simple multi-index model and under standard algorithmic and architectural choices.

</details>


### [228] [Physiological Trajectories and Observation Patterns in ICU Mortality Prediction: Transportability Under Changing Measurement Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42126)
*Benjamin T. Pike*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究区分生理轨迹与观察模式（测量频率/时序）在ICU死亡预测中的可迁移预测价值，并检验在固定观测数量下测量时序是否独立影响性能。结论是生理轨迹迁移更稳定，观察模式特征对测量时序与可获得性高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU死亡预测模型多关注跨人群迁移（新患者群体），但较少检验在“输入数据的测量条件”变化下是否仍稳健；同时难以区分性能提升来自生理轨迹信息本身，还是来自测量频率与时机这类观察模式（信息性缺失）特征。

Method: 基于MIMIC-III、MIMIC-IV与eICU，预测48小时院内死亡。构建嵌套表征：R1为最新值快照，R2加入纵向生理摘要，R3再加入测量频率与时序特征。以7,762名MetaVision患者训练，在21,682名MIMIC-IV患者及204家医院的43,878名eICU患者上外部验证；并通过计数匹配（固定保留观测数）的随机保留/随机删除实验，分离测量时序的独立效应，辅以bootstrap重拟合评估R3偏好比例及可用性降低情形下的表现变化。

Result: R2相对R1改善eICU外部预测（log-loss差-0.00922，95%CI -0.01148~-0.00704）。观察模式特征仅改善本地开发的eICU模型（R3-R2 -0.00257，95%CI -0.00370~-0.00146），从MetaVision迁移后不明确（-0.00012，95%CI -0.00206~+0.00208）；30次源域bootstrap重拟合中仅10次偏好R3。计数匹配下，保留分布在整个窗口内的测量相比随机保留降低log-loss：R3为0.03280，R2为0.00703；删除最后12小时相对于计数匹配随机删除增加log-loss：R3为0.01947，R2为0.00822。在测量可用性降低时，本地R3增益由有益转为有害。

Conclusion: 生理轨迹特征的预测增益迁移更为一致；观察模式（频率/时序）特征的增益依赖测量条件，跨中心迁移与观测缺失情形下不稳定甚至有害。临床预测模型除在新人群中评估外，还应在供给其输入的测量条件下进行评估。

Abstract: Objective: To distinguish the transferable predictive value of physiological trajectories from that of observation patterns in ICU mortality prediction, and to assess whether measurement timing affects performance independently of observation quantity.
  Materials and Methods: In-hospital mortality was predicted at 48 hours using MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV, and eICU. Nested representations progressively integrated latest-value snapshots (R1), longitudinal physiological summaries (R2), and measurement frequency and timing features (R3). Models fitted on 7,762 MetaVision patients were evaluated on 21,682 MIMIC-IV patients and 43,878 eICU patients across 204 hospitals. The effect of measurement timing was assessed while holding the number of retained observations constant.
  Results: Physiological trajectories improved external prediction in eICU (R2-R1 log-loss difference -0.00922, 95% CI -0.01148 to -0.00704). Observation-pattern features improved locally developed eICU models (R3-R2 -0.00257, 95% CI -0.00370 to -0.00146), but not clearly after transfer from MetaVision (-0.00012, 95% CI -0.00206 to +0.00208). R3 was favoured in 10 of 30 source bootstrap refits. At matched observation counts, retaining measurements spread throughout the window reduced log loss relative to random retention by 0.03280 for R3 versus 0.00703 for R2. Removing the final 12 hours increased log loss relative to count-matched random removal by 0.01947 for R3 versus 0.00822 for R2. Under reduced availability, the local R3 increment changed from beneficial to harmful.
  Discussion: Physiological trajectories transferred more consistently, while observation-pattern features were particularly sensitive to the timing and availability of measurements.
  Conclusion: Clinical prediction models should be evaluated not only in new patient populations but also under the measurement conditions that supply their inputs.

</details>


### [229] [Instance-dependent sequential limits as an exact information ledger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42133)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将bandit regret、序贯检验、最佳臂识别与MDP的instance-dependent下界统一写成一条"精确账本"链：散度分解→数据处理→统计量→检验→对替代与分配的优化，并量化每一步的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有下界证明链中只有散度分解是恒等式，其余步骤（数据处理、检验松弛、渐近等）都在暗中丢弃信息，导致难以定位标准算法与经典下界之间的差距来源。作者希望把整条链写成一个可逐项核算的"精确账本"。

Method: 以链式法则把数据处理步骤的损失刻画为恰好一个条件散度，并证明该散度在自然指数族下于逐臂计数与奖励和处消失；构造单参数鞅恒等式族，把散度分解推广到两个instance之间的任意tilt；随后用带命名缺陷的松弛得到各经典下界，并区分可由算法自身运行计算的缺陷与仅在类保证为数值时才可一致量化的缺陷。

Result: 在七篇已发表来源（从电影评分到发动机替换）上，账本定位了标准算法与经典下界之间的差异；在已训练模型间的识别任务与Wald检验中，最大项通常是"一致量化"缺陷。

Conclusion: 下界链中除渐近步外的缺陷均可由算法自身运行估计，从而把"理论下界与实测表现之差"分解为可核算、可归因的项；一致量化项在高斯型/检验型场景中主导差距。

Abstract: Instance-dependent limits for bandit regret, sequential tests, best-arm identification and Markov decision processes are proved by one chain. It runs from a divergence decomposition through data processing to a statistic, a testing step that turns a class guarantee into a requirement on the history, and optimization over alternatives and allocations. Only the first step is an identity. This work writes the chain as an exact ledger. By the chain rule, data processing loses exactly a conditional divergence, which vanishes for arms in natural exponential families at the per-arm counts and reward sums. A one-parameter family of martingale identities extends the decomposition to every tilt between two instances. The classical limits follow by relaxations with named defects. All defects but the asymptotic step's can be computed from an algorithm's own runs, the uniform quantification's only when the class guarantee is a number. On seven published sources, from movie ratings to engine replacement, the ledger locates the difference between standard algorithms and the classical limits. For identification among trained models and for Wald's test the largest term is usually the uniform quantification's.

</details>


### [230] [Counting on Dummies: Privacy Amplification with Observable Counts and Truncated Batches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42139)
*Vitaly Feldman,Audra McMillan,Kunal Talwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用随机数量的"dummy points（虚拟点）"对批大小进行隐私化处理的简单机制，从而在批次被截断时仍保持DP-SGD的隐私放大保证，并给出针对Poisson采样与随机分配的通用分析及紧致隐私会计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DP-SGD的实际部署中常用随机采样批次以获得隐私放大，但Poisson采样、随机分配等方案的批次大小可变，其隐私保证要求批大小不被泄露；分布式场景下服务器或通信观察者可获知批大小。同时内存与算力限制要求批大小不超过上限s，超限需截断，而已知截断会显著增大隐私损失。

Method: 引入一个简单的机制：使用随机数量的dummy points把批大小"隐私化"，即使发生截断也能维持隐私保证。该机制易于在分布式环境下实现；作者对Poisson采样与random allocation两种采样方案给出通用分析，设计成支持高效且相对紧致的隐私会计，并利用子采样对计数发布的隐私进行放大，使所需dummy points的期望数量渐近小于直接用非负噪声对（敏感度为1的）批大小做隐私化所需的数量。

Result: 在截断批次的Poisson采样设定下，实验表明该机制在隐私—效率权衡上优于Ganesh (2025) 近期给出的截断直接会计方法；同时，由于子采样放大，dummy points的期望数量渐近更少。

Conclusion: 通过随机dummy points对批大小进行隐私化，是一种既易于分布式实现又能兼容截断的通用手段，可在保持隐私放大保证的同时获得更优的隐私—效率折衷。

Abstract: In many practical deployments of Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD), the server processes data in batches. Randomly sampling these batches allows for better privacy bounds via privacy amplification results. Most sampling schemes with provable privacy amplification, including Poisson sampling and random allocation, result in variable batch sizes. The privacy guarantee of these schemes requires that the batch sizes are not revealed. In distributed settings, the server performing the aggregation or any party observing the communication traffic may learn these batch sizes. Furthermore, in both centralized and distributed settings, memory and computation constraints may require each batch to be of some maximum size $s$, necessitating truncation of the batch when it exceeds this limit. It is known that this truncation can result in a significant increase in privacy loss. In this work, we address both of these challenges via a simple mechanism that uses a random number of \emph{dummy points} to privatize the batch size and maintain the privacy guarantee even under truncation. We demonstrate that our mechanism is easy to implement in a distributed setting, and provide a general analysis of this mechanism for both Poisson sampling and random allocation. Our analysis is designed to support efficient and relatively tight privacy accounting. It also effectively amplifies the privacy of the count releases by subsampling, making the expected number of dummy points asymptotically smaller than what is needed to directly privatize the (sensitivity-one) batch sizes with non-negative noise. For Poisson sampling with truncated batches, we demonstrate empirically that our mechanism achieves better privacy--efficiency tradeoffs than the direct accounting for truncation recently given by (Ganesh, 2025).

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Explain, Then Forget: Recommendation Unlearning under Asymmetric Access Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40658)
*Haichao Zhang,Chong Zhang,Wen Wang,Shi Qiu,Peiyu Hu,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CEU框架，在MLaaS黑盒环境下通过基于因果解释的输出级分数干预实现推荐遗忘，无需修改模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐遗忘需去除噪声点击、过时偏好等历史交互的影响。但在MLaaS架构中，模型由模型提供方远程托管，服务运营方仅能通过API访问，无法获取或修改内部参数，导致依赖重训练/微调的现有遗忘方法难以适用。

Method: 两步式输出级分数干预：(1) 估计被遗忘交互对当前推荐的因果贡献；(2) 依据该因果信号调整受影响物品的最终排序分数，从而消除其影响，全程不触碰模型内部权重。因参数不变，可天然支持连续遗忘请求并避免重复参数更新带来的误差累积。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，CEU的遗忘效果与完全重训练相当，同时保持推荐效用并显著降低计算开销。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 输出级因果干预是MLaaS场景下推荐遗忘的可行且高效范式，兼顾遗忘充分性、推荐质量与低成本，并适配连续遗忘需求。

Abstract: Recommendation unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific historical interactions that should no longer affect future recommendations, such as noisy clicks or outdated user preferences. However, performing recommendation unlearning becomes much harder in modern Machine-Learning-as-a-Service (MLaaS) architectures, where the recommendation model is remotely hosted by a model provider (MP) and accessed by service operators (SO) only through APIs. In this setting, a service operator serves end users but cannot access or modify internal model parameters. As a result, existing unlearning methods are difficult to apply because they require access to model internals for retraining, fine-tuning, or other parameter updates. To address these challenges, we propose Causal Explanation-based Unlearning (CEU), a novel unlearning framework tailored for MLaaS environments. Instead of modifying internal model weights, CEU operates through a two-step output-level score intervention. First, it estimates how the forgotten interaction causally contributes to the current recommendation. Second, guided by this causal signal, the system removes the influence of the forgotten interaction by adjusting the final ranking scores of affected items. Because CEU leaves model parameters untouched, it naturally supports continuous unlearning requests while avoiding the error accumulation that can result from repeated parameter updates. Extensive experiments on three public datasets show that CEU achieves unlearning effectiveness comparable to full retraining while preserving recommendation utility and significantly reducing computational cost. The source code is available at: https://github.com/zhang-haichao/Explain-and-Forget.

</details>


### [232] [Retrieve-then-Disambiguate: Scalable Reviewer Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40768)
*Vicente Amado Olivo,Joe Petrecca,Wolfgang E. Kerzendorf*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出"检索-消歧"（retrieve-then-disambiguate）开源算法，将审稿人发现形式化为检索问题：对给定投稿检索相似文献，再将歧义作者名消解为唯一研究者身份，从而识别领域相关的潜在审稿人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学投稿量增长给同行评审带来压力，同时大量相关研究者未被识别、无法进入审稿人候选池，因此需要自动化地发现有领域相关性的研究者。

Method: 将研究者发现建模为检索问题：基于领域相似度检索与排序。算法分两步——先从科学文献中检索与查询投稿相似的出版物，再将模糊作者姓名消歧为唯一研究者身份。在500篇天文学与物理学论文上评估，以ORCID关联的第一作者、共同作者和被引研究者作为代理标签，衡量对已知关联研究者的召回。

Result: 在检索最相似的前500篇论文时，算法召回约68%的第一作者、60%的共同作者和45%的被引研究者。

Conclusion: 检索加姓名消歧的流程能有效发现与投稿领域相关的潜在审稿人，且代码开源（GitHub: deepthought-initiative/Retrieve-then-Disambiguate），为缓解审稿人发现瓶颈提供了可行方案；但召回率随关联强度减弱而下降，被引研究者召回相对较低。

Abstract: The growing number of scientific submissions is straining the peer review process, while a large pool of relevant researchers remains unidentified and is therefore not considered as potential reviewers. We formulate the discovery of these researchers as a retrieval problem: given a submission, we retrieve and rank researchers based on domain similarity. We present retrieve-then-disambiguate, an open-source algorithm that identifies domain relevant researchers for a query submission by retrieving similar publications from the scientific literature and resolving ambiguous author names into unique researcher identities. We evaluate the algorithm on 500 astronomy and physics publications. We use ORCID-linked first authors, coauthors, and cited researchers as proxy labels to evaluate the recovery of researchers with known connections to each query publication. When retrieving the top 500 most similar papers, the algorithm recovers roughly 68\% of first authors, 60\% of coauthors, and 45\% of cited researchers. Our code is available at https://github.com/deepthought-initiative/Retrieve-then-Disambiguate.

</details>


### [233] [ProdCrawl: A Replayable Test Collection for Focused Crawling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40855)
*Manuel Rey-Escobar,David Otero,Javier Parapar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 ProdCrawl——可复现的聚焦爬虫测试集合（621个网站、约110万静态镜像页面，用三个LLM多数投票标注产品页），使不同爬虫在同一页面与标签上公平比较；实验显示神经嵌入方法优于所有简单基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚焦爬虫通常在真实网站上以harvest rate评估，存在两大问题：网站随时间变化导致抓取无法在相同条件下重复，且缺乏“哪些页面相关”的ground truth，因而爬虫间的比较不可靠。

Method: 构建ProdCrawl：621个公司/机构网站的静态镜像，约110万页面，每页依据三个大语言模型的多数投票标注为产品页或非产品页；在该固定集合上运行各种爬虫并统一评分，比较广度优先、深度优先、文本分类器、关键词匹配、链接文本相似检索（含神经嵌入）等方法。

Result: 文本分类器与关键词匹配相对广度优先爬取无显著提升；基于语义比较链接文本的神经嵌入方法优于所有简单策略；由于每次运行都在完全相同的页面与标签上评估，结果可精确复现。

Conclusion: 静态镜像+LLM多数投票标注的测试集合可实现聚焦爬虫的公平、可复现比较；公开发布数据集、代码与模型以支持实验复现与后续研究。

Abstract: A focused crawler searches for web pages on a specific topic under a constrained download budget. Since it can only fetch a limited number of pages, its success depends on choosing which links to follow. Focused crawlers are usually evaluated on live websites by harvest rate, the fraction of downloaded pages that are relevant. This has two problems: on the one hand, websites change over time, so a crawl cannot be repeated under the same conditions; on the other hand, there is no ground truth for which pages are relevant. As a result, comparisons between crawlers are unreliable. We present ProdCrawl, a replayable test collection for focused crawling. It comprises static mirrors of 621 company and organization websites, about 1.1 million pages, with each page labeled as a product page or non-product page based on a majority vote among three large language models. Any crawler can be run on exactly the same sites and scored against the same labels. We use ProdCrawl to compare simple baselines (breadth-first, depth-first and text classifiers) with an approach that scores each link by searching for similar link texts whose target pages have already been labeled. Our results show that text classifiers and keyword matching do not significantly outperform breadth-first crawling, whereas neural embeddings, which compare link texts by semantic meaning, outperform all simple strategies. Because every run evaluates over identical pages and labels, these results are exactly reproducible. The collection, codebase, and models are publicly released to support experimental replication and fair crawler comparison.

</details>


### [234] [Context as Infrastructure: Knowledge Compilation for Retrieval in Agentic Systems with Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41172)
*Zhichang Yu,Baining Cao,Sbaaoui Idriss,Qi Wang,Gangqiang Xu,Ling Qin,Xiaoling Jin,An Liu,Shaohao Cai,Xiaoyang Bao,Shengle Chen,Changchang Ding,Pengfei Zhu,Jingxiang Xu,Yifeng Mao,Lu Liu,Hai Jin,Yingfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出“知识编译”（knowledge compilation）作为智能体式RAG的供给层：在强搜索harness保证准确率的前提下，用可配置的LLM增强式摄取管线（导航层级+claim-evidence叶子、实体图、时间线、思维导图、本体、wiki、记忆）为模型提供“最小充分上下文”，而非依赖更好的检索器。RAGFlow实现该管线与五站式harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体式RAG（写查询、读结果、改写、决定停止）已基本解决准确率问题（仅BM25/grep的harness在BrowseComp-Plus达85%、多跳FRAMES达91%，无需编译或向量索引），但暴露出上下文问题：无约束的循环会重复查询、重读文档、把窗口塞满无用段落。缺失的一层是知识编译。

Method: 构建可配置的摄取管线，用LLM为文档补充单个chunk无法提供的上下文（导航层级及其claim-evidence叶子、实体图、时间线、思维导图、本体、wiki、记忆）；核心机制是以claim-evidence对为叶子的导航树，并配以决策门（decision gate）判断证据是否充分以决定是否替换检索；同时将RAGFlow的agent循环规范为五个“站点”，控制进入模型的上下文为最小充分集合。

Result: 在合格harness下，结构化工件对FRAMES的准确率无可检测增益，PageIndex式大纲在大规模下退化；但claim-evidence导航树在证据充分时削减91%查询token，不充分时增加1.9%，平均降低38%，隐含2%的盈亏平衡充分率、实测约43%。决策门将延迟从140s降至30s；token节省以更多检索轮次和相应延迟为代价。本体、图、wiki、时间线等工件在治理、集成、上手、时序定位、连续性等部署场景中体现价值。

Conclusion: 知识编译不是更好的检索器，而是供给层：在强harness下不提升准确率，但显著压缩上下文与延迟。RAGFlow（开源）将循环规范为五个站点并输送编译化、受治理的上下文，形成“智能体原生数据库”——向agent提供编译好的上下文而非排序段落列表。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation is becoming agentic. A search harness writes queries, reads results, reformulates, and decides when to stop. This largely solves accuracy: a harness with only BM25/grep and MiniMax M3 answers 85% of BrowseComp-Plus and 91% on multi-hop FRAMES in our runs, without compiled or vector indexes. It exposes a context problem: an undisciplined loop repeats queries, rereads documents, and fills the window with unused passages. The missing layer is knowledge compilation: a configurable ingestion pipeline where LLMs enrich documents with context no chunk contains alone--navigation hierarchies with claim-evidence leaves, entity graphs, timelines, mind maps, ontologies, wikis, and memory. Compilation is not a better retriever: under a competent harness, structural artifacts gave no detectable accuracy gain on FRAMES, and a PageIndex-style outline degraded at scale. It is a supply layer. A navigation tree whose leaves are claim-evidence pairs--the mechanism we measure--cuts query tokens by 91% when evidence suffices and adds 1.9% when it does not, a 38% average reduction; this implies a 2% break-even sufficiency rate and a measured rate near 43%. A decision gate cuts latency from 140 s to 30 s, though substitution's token saving is bought with retrieval rounds and carries its own latency cost. Other artifacts--ontology, graph, wiki, timeline, mind map, memory--pay off in deployment (governance, integration, onboarding, temporal orientation, continuity) where accuracy benchmarks cannot see them. We describe RAGFlow's pipeline and harness (open source at https://github.com/infiniflow/ragflow), which turns the loop into five disciplined stations and keeps the context entering the model the smallest sufficient set; together they form an agent-native database: a store that hands the agent compiled, governed context instead of a ranked list of passages.

</details>


### [235] [MILES-Rec: Uncovering Implicit Intents from Mobility Behaviors for Cross-Domain POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41319)
*Yi Zou,Wei Luo,Xuewen Zhang,Zhuang Yan,Pengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 MILES-Rec 跨域 POI 推荐框架，从异构地图行为中推断潜在服务意图并表达为优先级排序的自然语言查询，配套 MILES-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地图应用中用户与工具的交互只能提供间接且不完整的消费需求证据，难以从观测行为直接推断个性化服务需求。

Method: 1) 从渐进式推理示范中学习，综合用户画像与地图历史完成情境理解、意图扩展与查询优先级排序；2) 提出意图对齐的组相对策略优化（GRPO），以下游用户交互为奖励，激励查询相关性与排序服务覆盖度，惩罚冗余与过长回复；3) 两级信用分配机制，将列表级共享优势与查询级优势结合，协调单条查询质量与整体查询列表的互补覆盖。

Result: 构建基于大规模地图平台真实交互日志的 MILES-Bench，覆盖丰富与稀疏行为历史下的多类服务；实验显示相较通用 LLM 与专用推荐基线，在 POI 检索效用、服务与语义对齐上均更优，且行为策略优化在四种骨干规模上带来一致增益。

Conclusion: 将 POI 推荐重构为意图推断与自然语言查询生成任务，并以交互反馈驱动的策略优化加以对齐，是提升跨域服务推荐的有效路径。

Abstract: Map applications bridge everyday mobility with local services, creating opportunities to recommend places by uncovering users' implicit intents. However, utility-driven map interactions provide only indirect and incomplete evidence of consumption demands, making it challenging to infer personalized service needs from observed behaviors. We propose MILES-Rec, a cross-domain POI recommendation framework that infers latent service intents from heterogeneous map behaviors and expresses them as prioritized natural language queries. It first learns from progressive reasoning demonstrations that synthesize user profiles and map histories for situation understanding, intent expansion, and query prioritization. We then introduce intent-aligned group relative policy optimization, using subsequent user interactions to reward query relevance and ranked service coverage while penalizing redundancy and excessive response length. A two-level credit assignment mechanism integrates shared list-level advantages with query-specific advantages, coordinating individual query quality with the complementary coverage of the complete query list. We further construct MILES-Bench from real user interaction logs on a large-scale mapping platform, supporting training and evaluation across diverse services under rich and sparse behavioral histories. Extensive experiments against general-purpose LLMs and specialized recommendation baselines demonstrate superior POI retrieval utility and strong service and semantic alignment, with consistent gains from behavioral policy optimization across four backbone scales.

</details>


### [236] [Pix2Evidence: A Multimodal RAG System for Text and Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41335)
*Ziniu Liu,Xiaoyang Chen,Dezhi Ye,Jie Liu,Jin Ma,Hao Deng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Pix2Evidence 是一个多模态 RAG 系统，将截图切片与其转录文本融合为单一图文表示进行检索，并用视觉语言教师生成证据描述来适配检索器与重排序，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对视觉丰富文档做 RAG 时，既要找到与问题相关的内容，又要定位支撑答案的证据。截图检索虽保留图像与版面结构，但截图中的文字事实仍需可检索；且仅凭主题相关性无法判断哪张检索到的切片真正包含答题证据。

Method: 提出 Pix2Evidence：将每个截图切片与其可用转录文本编码为统一的图像—文本融合表示，使版面与可读文本在检索中被联合考虑；再用视觉语言教师模型为每个训练问题描述其可见支撑内容，以此适配检索器，并在答案生成前对候选进行重排序。

Result: 在五个常用 Wikipedia QA 基准上，未重排序的 Pix2Evidence 在四个基准的答案准确率上领先主对比；在 MMSearch 上达到 26.0%，比 PixelRAG 高 4.4 个百分点。

Conclusion: 融合图文表示并引入证据导向的检索适配与重排序，可有效提升视觉丰富文档 RAG 的证据定位与问答表现，优于现有最先进方法；代码与衍生数据将在许可与隐私审查允许范围内公开。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over visually rich documents must find both relevant content and the evidence that supports an answer. Screenshot retrieval preserves images and page structure, but written facts within screenshots also need to be searchable. Moreover, topical relevance alone does not identify which retrieved tile contains evidence for the answer. We introduce Pix2Evidence, a multimodal RAG system that encodes each screenshot tile together with its available transcription into a single fused image--text representation, so that layout and readable text are considered jointly during retrieval. To guide evidence selection, a vision-language teacher describes the visible content that supports each training question. We use these descriptions to adapt the retriever, then rerank the retrieved candidates before answer generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Pix2Evidence outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Across five widely-used Wikipedia QA benchmarks, the non-reranked Pix2Evidence leads the main comparison in answer accuracy on four. On MMSearch, it reaches 26.0\%, 4.4 percentage points above PixelRAG. We will release the code and share derived data where licensing and privacy reviews permit.

</details>


### [237] [Defining Page-Centric Visual Document Retrieval and Evaluating Existing Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41360)
*Chongzhi Zhang,Yuqi Zuo,Aixin Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文提出以页面为索引单元的"页面级视觉文档检索"(VDR)形式化框架，区分页面/文档两种粒度与独立条目、证据集、迭代三种检索范式，并系统评析42个基准，指出当前VDR评估集中于页面级独立条目检索、真实用户需求型查询与分级相关性标注稀缺，故明确提出面向未来基准设计的优先方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代IR中的文档日益呈现为结构化、视觉丰富、分页的单元（Word、PDF、幻灯片），而纯文本检索系统要么忽视该结构，要么通过任意段落切分将其破坏；同时应用所需能力与现有评估协议所能确立的结论之间存在明显错位，亟需厘清VDR的任务形式与评测现状。

Method: 1) 形式化页面中心VDR：以页面为基本索引单元，文档上下文用于判定页面相关性；区分页面级与文档级两种检索粒度，划分独立条目、证据集、迭代三种检索范式。2) 构建评估框架：以8项准则（语料特征、查询构造、相关性标注、排序支持等）对42个基准进行结构化评析。

Result: 揭示两大发现：(1) 评估仍集中于页面级独立条目检索，支持文档级、证据集与迭代设置的资源较少；(2) 源于真实用户信息需求、独立于目标语料内容构造的查询以及分级相关性判断均不常见，相关性标注的覆盖度与核验程度差异显著。

Conclusion: 现有评估协议尚不足以确立应用所需的VDR能力。论文提供基准选择指南，并指出未来基准设计的优先事项，强调任务需求、相关性语义与评估协议三者需保持一致。

Abstract: In modern information retrieval (IR), documents are increasingly encountered as structured sequences of visually rich, paginated units, rather than as flat text. Example documents encountered in our daily life include Word files, PDFs, and slide decks. Free text-centric retrieval systems either ignore this structure entirely or destroy it through arbitrary passage chunking. In this paper, we formulate page-centric Visual Document Retrieval (VDR) with pages as the fundamental indexing units and document context available for assessing page relevance. The formulation distinguishes two retrieval granularities, page and document, and three retrieval paradigms: independent-item, evidence-set, and iterative retrieval. We then assess 42 benchmarks using eight criteria covering corpus characteristics, query construction, relevance annotation, and ranking support. Our analysis shows two main findings on current VDR research. First, evaluation remains concentrated on page-level independent-item retrieval, with fewer resources supporting document-level, evidence-set, and iterative settings. Second, queries originating from real user information needs independently of target corpus content and graded relevance judgments are uncommon, while the coverage and verification of relevance judgments vary substantially. Both findings reveal gaps between the capabilities applications require and those existing evaluation protocols can establish. We provide benchmark selection guidance and identify priorities for future benchmark design, emphasizing alignment between task requirements, relevance semantics, and evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [238] [BrowseFlow: Sparse Event Flow Matching for Generative User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41374)
*Yitian Pang,Jiayi Zhang,Zhenjia Ma,Xiaowei Yue,Dagui Chen,Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BrowseFlow：条件离散流匹配的用户浏览模拟方法，按"浏览深度+关键事件序列"建模，解决随机响应与关键事件稀疏两大问题，并给出能量分数评价与TaobaoBrowse基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
